Abstract: Recognizing new and rare words - named entities, acronyms, domain specific special words, and other items scarce in training data - remains a key challenge for automatic speech recognition (ASR). We compare two strategies for this: context biasing methods, where an ASR model is extended such that during inference a word list can be supplied, and speech large language models (LLMs) prompted with context directly. We evaluate two context biasing methods based on Whisper against three speech LLMs across read and non-read speech, reporting biased, unbiased, and overall word error rate (WER). The context biasing methods cut biased WER by up to 88% relative while leaving other words largely unaffected. Speech LLMs excel on read speech but generalize less well to non-read speech, and prove sensitive to distractor count and prompt word order. We characterize the resulting trade-offs to guide method selection.

</details>


### [37] [Task-Conditional Flow Matching for Balanced Multilingual Text Embedding Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05785)
*Tirth Bhatt,Naren Kumar S,Mayank Singh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务条件流匹配（TCFM）框架，用于多语言文本嵌入模型的微调。TCFM针对不同任务采用不同的优化策略：翻译任务使用流匹配，而检索、分类和配对分类任务则使用与其学习动态更匹配的目标函数。同时结合教师引导的表示保留和三阶段课程学习实现稳定适配，在Indic MTE基准上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语言文本嵌入模型在不同任务上使用单一训练目标进行适配，但不同任务本质上需要不同的优化策略，单一目标难以在所有任务上取得最佳性能。

Method: TCFM框架包含三个核心设计：(1) 任务条件化优化——翻译任务使用Flow Matching，其他任务（检索、分类、配对分类）使用与各自学习动态更匹配的目标函数；(2) 教师引导的表示保存——通过教师模型约束嵌入表示稳定性；(3) 三阶段课程学习——分阶段逐步提高训练难度以确保稳定适配。

Result: 在Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark上评估，TCFM取得新的最先进结果，持续提升多语言任务上的嵌入质量，并跨不同嵌入模型家族具有良好泛化能力。

Conclusion: TCFM通过任务条件化的优化策略有效解决了多任务适配中单一目标函数的局限性，验证了为不同任务定制优化方案在多语言嵌入适配中的有效性，未来将公开代码和数据集以促进研究。

Abstract: Multilingual text embedding models are commonly adapted using a single training objective across diverse tasks, despite different tasks requiring fundamentally different optimization strategies. We introduce Task-Conditional Flow Matching (TCFM), a multilingual embedding adaptation framework that selectively applies Flow Matching to translation tasks while optimizing retrieval, classification, and pair-classification tasks with objectives better aligned to their learning dynamics. TCFM further combines teacher-guided representation preservation with a three-stage curriculum to enable stable adaptation. Evaluated on the Indic Massive Text Embedding Benchmark, TCFM establishes a new state-of-the-art, consistently improving embedding quality across a diverse set of multilingual tasks and generalizing across embedding model families. We will publicly release the codebase and datasets upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [38] [On-Policy Delta Distillation for Multilingual Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05802)
*Byeongho Heo,Jaehui Hwang,Sangdoo Yun,Dongyoon Han*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于策略蒸馏（OPD）及其高级变体OPD²在英语、韩语和日语数学推理任务中的表现。实验表明，OPD²在韩语和日语上显著优于原始OPD，并能缩小英语-韩语性能差距；同时发现仅用英语数据训练OPD虽能提升韩日语性能，但会导致响应向英语偏移，凸显多语言数据的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD作为LLM后训练中强化学习的有力替代方案正崭露头角，但其在多语言场景下的有效性尚未被充分探索。作者旨在填补这一研究空白，系统评估OPD及其变体在多语言数学推理任务中的表现。

Method: 采用Qwen3模型进行实验，对比原始OPD和OPD²（利用后训练教师模型与其基础模型之间的概率差距作为学习信号）在英语、韩语和日语数学推理任务上的效果。同时设计实验检验仅使用英语数据进行OPD训练对多语言性能的影响。

Result: OPD²在所有语言上均优于原始OPD，在韩语和日语上提升尤为显著，且总体上缩小了英语-韩语的性能差距。此外，仅英语数据的OPD也能提升韩语和日语的性能，但会导致响应向英语偏移。

Conclusion: OPD²是多语言LLM后训练中比原始OPD更优的方案，尤其适用于非英语语言。多语言数据对于保持目标语言响应至关重要，仅依赖英语数据会引入语言偏移问题。

Abstract: On-Policy Distillation (OPD) is emerging as a promising alternative to reinforcement learning for LLM post-training, yet its effectiveness in multilingual settings remains underexplored. We study OPD and its advanced variant, On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$), for mathematical reasoning in English, Korean, and Japanese. OPD$^2$ improves OPD by using the probability gap between a post-trained teacher and its base model as the learning signal. Experiments with Qwen3 show that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms the original OPD, with particularly strong improvements in Korean and Japanese, and generally narrows the English-Korean performance gap. We further find that English-only OPD can also increase performance for Korean and Japanese, but often shifts the responses toward English, highlighting the importance of multilingual data to preserving target-language responses.

</details>


### [39] [Hierarchical Latent Prediction for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05806)
*Chang Shi,Tim Pearce,Manan Tomar,Siddhartha Sen,John Langford*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出层次化潜在预测（HiLP），通过引入辅助的高层抽象潜在变量来减少潜在空间滚动中的错误累积效应，从而改善语言模型的长期推理和规划能力。实验表明HiLP在编码和多步推理基准上表现优异，并提升了投机解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的下一令牌预测（NTP）基于教师强制训练范式，对长期推理和规划可能并非最优。已有的多令牌预测（MTP）和下一潜在预测（NextLat）虽试图缓解该问题，但前者视野有限，后者在多步滚动中遭受误差累积问题。因此需要一种方法来解决潜在空间滚动中的错误累积。

Method: HiLP引入一个辅助的高层抽象潜在变量，作为中间层级的表征来帮助减少潜在空间多步滚动中的误差累积效应，从而支持更长距离的连贯信念状态表示。

Result: 实验表明HiLP能够产生更长视界的连贯信念状态表示，在编码和多步推理基准上验证了方法的有效性，并且提供了更高效的投机解码性能。

Conclusion: HiLP通过层次化的潜在预测策略有效缓解了潜在空间滚动中的误差累积问题，为语言模型在长期推理和规划任务上的预训练提供了更优的辅助目标设计。

Abstract: While standard Next-Token Prediction (NTP) lays the foundation of language model pre- training, its teacher-forced training paradigm may not be optimal for long-horizon reasoning and planning. Recent works such as Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) and Next-Latent prediction (NextLat) try to mitigate the problem through predicting multiple future tokens and self-supervised prediction in the latent space. However, those auxiliary objectives either have a limited horizon or suffer from compounding error from multi-step rollout. We introduce Hierarchical Latent Prediction (HiLP), which introduces an auxiliary higher-level abstract latent to help reduce the error accumulation effect in latent-space rollouts. Experiments show that HiLP can lead to longer-horizon coherent belief state representation and demonstrate the effectiveness of our method across coding and multi-step reasoning benchmarks, and offers more speculative decoding efficiency.

</details>


### [40] [M$^3$R-Bench: A Unified Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Metaphor Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05817)
*Hong Jiang,Junnan Zhu,Jingwang Huang,Xiao Sun,Yuming Yang,Jiang Zhong,Ruirui Chen,Jingman Shi,Hao Wu,Nayu Liu,Xinyi Jiang,Kaiwen Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出M³R-Bench，一个包含1,000个图文实例的统一、基于证据的隐喻理解基准，并提供隐喻出现、目标-源映射、情感及分阶段解释的联合标注。同时提出M³R-Reasoner模型，结合基于课程推理监督与任务感知强化学习，仅用8B参数即在统一任务指标上超越更大规模专有MLLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐喻理解基准主要通过孤立子任务评估模型能力，缺乏基于证据的解释，难以判断模型是否真正建立了基于视觉和文本线索的目标-源映射。作者旨在解决跨模态隐喻理解评估中缺乏统一基准和证据支持的问题。

Method: （1）构建M³R-Bench：基于概念隐喻理论及非字面语言理解理论，对1,000个图文实例进行人工验证标注，涵盖隐喻发生、目标-源映射、情感以及"证据识别—映射建立—情感推理"三阶段解释；（2）提出M³R-Reasoner：结合课程式推理监督与任务感知强化学习，使模型推理与隐喻理解对齐。

Result: 实验表明，现有模型常忽视视觉证据、依赖表层文本线索，产生不准确的目标-源映射。M³R-Reasoner仅以8B参数骨架，在四项统一任务指标上超越更大规模的专有MLLM，其中在视觉证据和情感论证得分上比GPT-5.5分别提升28.45和30.11分，在平均评分标准上超越Claude-Sonnet-4.6达8.00分。

Conclusion: M³R-Bench为多模态隐喻理解提供了统一、基于证据的评估框架，揭示了现有模型存在跨模态证据-映射不匹配的问题；M³R-Reasoner通过课程式推理监督和任务感知强化学习有效改善了这一缺陷，验证了小规模模型在隐喻理解任务上的潜力。

Abstract: Metaphor enables the understanding of abstract concepts through cross-domain mappings while conveying affective attitudes. In multimodal scenarios, visual and textual information jointly construct Target--Source mappings, requiring both conceptual understanding and cross-modal reasoning. However, existing benchmarks mainly evaluate metaphor understanding through isolated subtasks and lack evidence-grounded explanations, making it difficult to assess whether models establish mappings grounded in visual and textual cues.To address these limitations, we introduce M$^3$R-Bench, a unified and evidence-grounded benchmark containing 1,000 image--text instances with human-verified annotations. Guided by Conceptual Metaphor Theory and theories of nonliteral language understanding, M$^3$R-Bench provides joint annotations for metaphor occurrence, Target--Source mapping, sentiment, and stage-wise explanations following ``evidence identification--mapping establishment--sentiment inference.''Evaluations on M$^3$R-Bench reveal that existing models often overlook visual evidence, rely on superficial textual cues, and produce inaccurate Target--Source mappings, exposing a cross-modal evidence--mapping mismatch. To address this mismatch, we propose M$^3$R-Reasoner, which combines curriculum-based reasoning supervision with task-aware reinforcement learning to align model reasoning with metaphor interpretation. Experiments show that, with only an 8B-parameter backbone, M$^3$R-Reasoner outperforms larger proprietary MLLMs across four unified-task metrics and improves Visual Evidence and Sentiment Justification scores over GPT-5.5 by 28.45 and 30.11 points, respectively, while surpassing Claude-Sonnet-4.6 by 8.00 points in mean rubric score. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/hongshi4/M3R-Bench.

</details>


### [41] [Decomposed Entailment for Factuality Checking and Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05823)
*Achir Oukelmoun,Nasredine Semmar,Gaël De Chalendar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HallDetect是一个轻量级、免参考、黑盒的幻觉检测框架，通过将生成内容分解为原子声明并使用紧凑的编码器蕴含模型进行验证，在消费级硬件预算下优于同资源基线，并能在三个数据集上超越生成式和嵌入式的对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLM）常因幻觉而产生事实不一致的内容，即生成内容缺乏底层来源支持，这严重损害了其可靠性。现有幻觉检测方法往往依赖大规模生成模型或参考文本，计算成本高且应用场景受限，亟需一种轻量、高效且无需参考的检测方案。

Method: HallDetect基于分解式事实性评估：先将生成内容分解为原子声明，每个声明通过一个紧凑的基于编码器的蕴含模型进行验证，该模型采用对比式公式，在多种尺度的源文本块库上进行匹配；最后通过非对称聚合得分进行判定——即单个被高置信度反驳的声明即可标记整个回复为幻觉。该方法完全免参考（reference-free）且为黑盒，所有方法共享相同的4-bit量化骨干网络和消费级硬件预算。

Result: 在受控实验协议下（所有方法共享相同的4-bit量化骨干和消费级硬件预算），HallDetect在四个基准中的三个上优于同等资源的生成式和基于嵌入的基线方法，并且在不同的骨干网络家族上表现稳定。此外，该方法能生成从声明到原文片段的审计轨迹，定位每个错误的具体位置。

Conclusion: HallDetect验证了轻量级、免参考的幻觉检测方法的可行性，在保持高效计算的同时实现了优异的检测性能，并提供可解释的错误定位能力，为LLM幻觉检测提供了更经济实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is often compromised by factual inconsistencies, including hallucinations---cases where generated content is not supported by the underlying source. We present HallDetect, a lightweight, reference-free, and black-box framework for hallucination detection that we evaluate not only on summarization but across a broader range of source-grounded generation settings. HallDetect builds on decomposition-based factuality evaluation: generated content is decomposed into atomic claims, each verified by a compact encoder-based entailment model through a contrastive formulation over a multi-scale library of source chunks, and aggregated with an asymmetric score in which a single confidently contradicted claim flags the response. Under a controlled protocol in which all methods share the same 4-bit quantized backbones and consumer-grade hardware budget, HallDetect outperforms comparably resourced generative and embedding-based baselines on three of four benchmarks while remaining stable across backbone families, and yields a claim-to-span audit trail that localizes each error.

</details>


### [42] [MoCA: Implicit Social Context Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05825)
*Wenhao Xu,Kaiwen Zhang,Hao Li,Maowei You,Yongzheng Ji,Siyuan Zuo,Jingxuan Yu,Sina A,Xinyao Tan,Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了隐式社交情境分析任务（MoCA），从情感、意图和立场三个维度系统建模隐式社交场景，构建了包含3,108个多模态实例的高质量基准数据集，并提出了基于认知冲突的溯因推理框架（CoDAR），显著提升了多模态大模型在隐式社交理解上的表现，但仍与人类推理存在较大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类社交沟通（如情感和意图）常以高度隐式的方式传达，潜在含义通过间接、社会和文化层面的信号表达而非直白陈述。这类隐式社交情境在现实交互中普遍存在，但缺乏正式、系统的研究框架。现有大模型过度依赖显式线索，难以对潜在社交情境进行推理。

Method: 1) 提出MoCA任务，从情感（affection）、意图（intent）、立场（stance）三个关键维度系统建模隐式社交场景；2) 构建包含3,108个多模态实例的高质量基准，附带细粒度认知标注，揭示谁向谁表达了什么、如何以及为何传达；3) 提出CoDAR框架，将观察到的表达与期望的真实行为之间的差异建模为认知冲突，从而推断隐藏的心理状态。

Result: 实验表明，最先进的多模态大语言模型在MoCA任务上表现显著不足，因为它们依赖显式线索且对潜在社交情境推理能力有限。CoDAR框架能够大幅提升模型性能，但模型表现与人类推理之间仍存在较大差距。

Conclusion: 隐式社交理解对现代AI系统而言仍是根本性难题。CoDAR通过认知冲突驱动的溯因推理提供了有效改进方向，但人类与模型之间的性能差距表明，更深层次的隐式社交推理能力仍需进一步探索。

Abstract: Human social communication, such as affection and intent, is often conveyed in highly implicit ways, where underlying meanings are expressed through indirect, socially and culturally grounded signals rather than explicit statements. Such implicit social contexts are pervasive in real-world interactions, yet there remains a lack of a formal and systematic framework for studying them. In this paper, we introduce Implicit Social Context Analysis (MoCA), a novel task that systematically models implicit social scenarios along three key dimensions: affection, intent, and stance. We construct a high-quality benchmark containing 3,108 multimodal instances collected from real-world sources, with fine-grained cognitive annotations revealing who expresses what toward whom, as well as how and why it is conveyed. Using the MoCA dataset, we show that state-of-the-art multimodal large language models struggle significantly with this task because of their reliance on explicit cues and limited ability to reason over latent social contexts. To address this challenge, we propose Conflict-Driven Abductive Reasoning (CoDAR), a novel framework that models the discrepancy between observed expressions and expected truthful behavior as cognitive conflict, thereby enabling the inference of hidden mental states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoDAR substantially improves model performance. Nevertheless, a large gap from human reasoning remains, highlighting the fundamental difficulty of implicit social understanding.

</details>


### [43] [Enhancing Social Intelligence in LLMs with Hierarchical Reasoning and Utterance-Level Goal Rewarding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05832)
*Xiaofeng Wang,Kakam Chong,Shuai Xiao,DeXin Kong,Qingyuan Tian,Chen Ju,Xu Yan,Shuai Zhao,Fei Huang,Rui Wang,Shuguang Han,jufeng chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TSR框架，将社交对话分解为高层策略规划与低层语言执行两个层级，并引入LHRL-VGR算法动态分配奖励，在SOTOPIA基准上将7B模型微调后在目标完成率上超越GPT-4o基线7.32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在结构化任务中表现出色，但在需要长期目标协调和快速适应的动态社交交互中表现不佳。现有方法对所有话语施加统一的目标导向奖励，忽视了每轮对话目标的特异性，也未考虑潜在策略的合理性依据。

Method: 受计划行为理论启发，提出TSR（Think-Strategy-Response）框架，将社交对话分解为两个层级：高层策略规划与低层语言执行。引入LHRL-VGR（线性化分层强化学习与方差门控奖励）算法，根据目标达成分数的方差动态路由奖励，在目标完成与策略遵循之间取得平衡。

Result: 在SOTOPIA基准上，该方法将Qwen2.5-7B智能体微调后，目标完成成功率超越GPT-4o基线7.32%，在多智能体社交协商任务中达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 分层策略规划与动态奖励分配相结合的方法，能有效提升大语言模型在复杂社交交互中的表现，证明了将心理学理论（计划行为理论）融入强化学习用于社交对话优化的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel in structured tasks but struggle with dynamic social interactions, where success requires long-term goal coordination and rapid adaptation. Current methods often apply uniform goal-based rewards to every utterance, overlooking the specificity of objectives at each dialogue turn and failing to account for the rationale of potential strategies. Inspired by the Theory of Planned Behavior, we propose the Think-Strategy-Response (TSR) framework, which decomposes social dialogue into two hierarchical stages: high-level strategic planning and low-level linguistic execution. To optimize TSR, we introduce Linearized Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Variance-Gated Rewards (LHRL-VGR), a novel algorithm that dynamically routes rewards - balancing goal completion and strategy adherence - based on the variance of goal achievement scores. Experiments on the SOTOPIA benchmark show that our approach fine-tunes a Qwen2.5-7B agent to surpass the GPT-4o baseline by 7.32% in goal completion success, demonstrating state-of-the-art performance in multi-agent social negotiation tasks.

</details>


### [44] [MameLoshnLM: Yiddish Language Model and Evaluation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05850)
*Uri Katz,Omer Goldman,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Reut Tsarfaty,Noah A. Smith*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MameLoshnLM，这是首个专门为意第绪语构建的开源8B参数语言模型，并配套引入了高质量预训练语料库Oytser和多任务基准Kashes，在多项任务上超越了同等规模的开源基座模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 意第绪语虽然拥有丰富的文本传统，但其数字化资源稀缺、可靠评估资源匮乏，现有多语言语料库和基准存在大量噪声、机器翻译和质量低劣的文本，导致该语言的语言模型进展受限。

Result: 在Kashes基准的所有任务上，MameLoshnLM优于同等规模的开源基线模型；定量和定性分析均表明其更好地捕捉了意第绪语的词汇和形态学模式，优于通用多语言模型。

Conclusion: 研究结果为意第绪语NLP奠定了坚实基础，也为历史丰富但数字代表性不足的语言开发语言模型提供了可复用的实践模板；同时揭示了大规模有噪多语言数据对低资源语言的普遍失败模式。

Abstract: We present MameLoshnLM, the first open-source 8B-parameter language model built specifically for Yiddish. Despite Yiddish's rich textual tradition, its limited digital presence and the scarcity of reliable evaluation resources have constrained progress in Yiddish language modeling. Existing multilingual corpora and benchmarks are often poor proxies for the language, containing substantial amounts of noisy, machine-translated, and misclassified text. We address these gaps by introducing Oytser, a high-quality Yiddish pretraining corpus that combines contemporary web-native sources with literary materials, and Kashes, a multi-task benchmark spanning translation, linguistic analysis, information extraction, and language understanding. Using these resources, we continue pretraining Llama 3.1 8B to obtain MameLoshnLM. Across the tasks in the benchmark, MameLoshnLM outperforms open baselines of similar scale. Our analyses show that these gains are not only quantitative: relative to general-purpose multilingual models, MameLoshnLM better captures language-defining lexical and morphological patterns, pointing to a broader failure mode of noisy web-scale multilingual data for low-resource languages. Our results provide both a foundation for Yiddish NLP and a practical template for language model development in historically rich but digitally underrepresented languages.

</details>


### [45] [Mapping Similarity Spaces across Embedding Models with Synthetic Query Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05857)
*Marcin Rozmus,Peter van der Putten*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Synthetic Query Probing方法，通过从文档生成查询来创建受控的查询-块对，从而无需标签即可大规模分析不同嵌入模型之间相似度分数的分布关系。实验表明模型间排名基本一致但绝对分数存在系统性偏差，学习分数映射（尤其等距回归）可部分对齐分数空间并提高阈值在不同模型间的可移植性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统依赖相似度分数检索内容，但不同嵌入模型的几何性质不同导致分数不可直接比较，这使模型迁移变得困难并限制了阈值在不同模型间的复用。现有研究缺乏参考（reference-free）的大规模跨模型相似度行为分析方法。

Method: 提出Synthetic Query Probing框架：从文档中生成查询以构建受控的查询-块对，实现无参考的大规模跨模型相似度分析。使用线性回归、等距回归（isotonic regression）和分位数映射三种函数来学习分数转换关系。

Result: 在SciFact和专有语料库上的实验表明：不同嵌入模型在排名上大体一致，但绝对分数存在系统性失真。学到的映射函数能部分对齐分数空间并提升阈值可移植性，其中等距回归表现最佳。

Conclusion: 研究凸显了跨模型校准的必要性，并将Synthetic Query Probing定位为一种可扩展的嵌入可比性分析框架。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation systems rely on similarity scores to retrieve relevant content, yet scores are not directly comparable across embedding models due to differing geometric properties, complicating model migration and limiting threshold reuse. We study how similarity scores can be related by learning mappings between score distributions rather than embeddings. We introduce Synthetic Query Probing, generating queries from documents to create controlled query-chunk pairs, enabling large-scale, reference-free analysis of cross-model similarity behavior. We evaluate the approach on multiple embedding configurations and learn score conversion functions using linear, isotonic, and quantile mappings. Experiments on SciFact and a proprietary corpus show that while models largely agree on rankings, their absolute scores exhibit systematic distortions. Learned mappings partially align these spaces and improve threshold portability, with isotonic regression performing best. Our results highlight the need for cross-model calibration and position Synthetic Query Probing as a scalable framework for analyzing embedding comparability.

</details>


### [46] [MACRO: Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05872)
*Paweł Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Akylgali Aitaza,Przemysław Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MACRO框架，将LLM的层路由建模为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，在不修改模型参数的情况下学习任务特定的执行路径（支持跳过、重复和残差加法操作），通过训练数据反馈更新路由分布并用top-k Viterbi解码，在多个推理和知识基准上平均提升5.0%，相比最优动态路由方法Dr. LLM提升7.2%，且路由搜索时间减少9.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LLM按顺序逐层执行，而动态层路由（如层重复、跳过等操作）可提升性能。但现有路由方法存在三大局限：需要更新模型权重、每测试实例需昂贵搜索循环、或推理时依赖真实标签。为此需要一种无需修改参数、高效且无监督的层路由方法。

Method: MACRO将层路由形式化为上下文相关的马尔可夫策略，其状态条件包括：层索引、计算预算阶段、方向位移和算子上下文。支持的算子包括skip、repeat和残差隐藏状态加法。路由分布通过训练数据的反馈信号更新，最终使用top-k Viterbi算法解码出高概率的候选程序（路由路径）。整个过程无需修改底层模型参数。

Result: 在多个开源LLM和多样化推理/知识基准上，MACRO相比未路由基线平均提升+5.0%准确率，小模型上收益最大；相比最优基线Dr. LLM提升+7.2%，同时路由搜索时间从14.8小时降至1.6小时（减少9.4倍）。

Conclusion: MACRO证明了在不修改模型参数的前提下，通过马尔可夫策略学习任务特定的层路由可以有效提升LLM性能，且相比现有方法在精度和效率上均具有显著优势，代码已开源。

Abstract: Standard Large Language Models (LLMs) execute layers sequentially. Dynamic layer routing, i.e. search for a different execution path through layers involving layer repetitions, skips and other moves, can improve performance. Existing routing approaches often require updating model weights, running expensive search loops per test instance, or demand ground-truth labels during inference. In this work, we propose Markov Chain Routing of Transformer Layers (MACRO), a framework that learns task-specific routes over LLM architectures without modifying underlying parameters. MACRO models layer routing as a context-dependent Markov policy conditioned on layer indices, computation budget phases, directional displacements, and operator context, supporting skip, repeat, and residual hidden-state addition operations. The Markov route distribution is updated via feedback on training data and decoded using a top-k Viterbi algorithm to isolate high-probability candidate programs. We evaluate MACRO across diverse reasoning and knowledge benchmarks on multiple open-weight LLMs. MACRO achieves a +5.0% average accuracy improvement over the unrouted baselines, with largest gains on small models. We outperform the best dynamic routing approach Dr. LLM by +7.2%, while reducing route-search time 9.4x (from 14.8 to 1.6 hours). Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Batorskq/MACRO.

</details>


### [47] [Causal Episodic Memory for Feedback-Driven Agent Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05906)
*Khang Nhat Hoang Vo,Tam Minh Chu,Anh Trac Duc Dinh,Thuyen Vinh Ha Bui,Tho Quan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MERIT是一个免训练的Text-to-SQL修复智能体，通过维护在线双极性记忆（含验证过的成功修复和观察到的失败方向），在无参数更新的情况下利用先前episode的修复成果提升后续修复性能。在Spider上执行准确率从66.34%提升至69.79%，在BIRD上从47.35%提升至48.44%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在修复失败时往往丢弃成功的修正结果，导致后续episode需要重新探索相似的解决方案，造成计算和推理资源的浪费。作者希望研究能否在不更新模型参数的情况下，将已完成的修复结果用于改进后续Text-to-SQL任务。

Method: MERIT采用免训练架构，维护一个在线双极性记忆系统，包含oracle验证过的成功修正（positive memory）和观察到的失败方向（negative memory）。在oracle辅助的基准反馈下，仅检索先前已完成episode的记忆。一个确定性分类器先对失败类型进行分类，该分类结果作为条件引导混合的词法-稠密检索器，最后由冻结的模型生成每次修订。

Result: 使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct且保持相同初始预测和修复预算下，MERIT在Spider上将执行准确率从66.34%提升至69.79%（+3.45%），在BIRD上从47.35%提升至48.44%（+1.09%）。配对分析显示Spider上的增益有强统计证据，但BIRD上的证据较弱。MERIT与无类型条件的动态检索在任一基准上均无可靠区分。Reflexion式记忆在BIRD上达到51.24%但推理成本显著更高。消融实验显示：负面记忆贡献有限，类型条件和词法-稠密排序的价值取决于数据集，schema局部经验提供最一致的收益。

Conclusion: 因果跨查询记忆在Text-to-SQL修复中的效果存在场景依赖性：在schema相对简单、候选记忆更相关的场景（如Spider）中增益显著；而在更复杂、更广泛的场景（如BIRD）中，更宽泛的记忆表示（如Reflexion式）仍具优势。研究结果厘清了何时基于跨查询因果记忆的修复有效，何时更广泛的记忆表示更受青睐。

Abstract: LLM agents that repair failures often discard successful corrections, forcing later episodes to rediscover similar solutions. We study whether finalized repair outcomes can improve subsequent Text-to-SQL episodes without parameter updates. We introduce MERIT, a training-free agent that maintains an online dual-polarity memory of oracle-verified corrections and observed unsuccessful directions. Under oracle-assisted benchmark feedback, only memories from earlier finalized episodes are eligible for retrieval. A deterministic classifier assigns a coarse failure type, which conditions a hybrid lexical-dense retriever before the frozen model generates each revision. Using Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct with identical initial predictions and repair budgets, MERIT improves execution accuracy over stateless iterative repair from \(66.34\%\) to \(69.79\%\) on Spider and from \(47.35\%\) to \(48.44\%\) on BIRD. Paired analyses provide clear evidence for the Spider gain but weaker evidence on BIRD. MERIT is not reliably separated from untyped dynamic retrieval on either benchmark, while Reflexion-style memory reaches \(51.24\%\) on BIRD at substantially higher inference cost. Ablations show that negative memory contributes modestly, the value of type conditioning and lexical--dense ranking is dataset dependent, and schema-local experience provides the most consistent benefit. These results clarify when causal cross-query memory improves repair and when broader memory representations remain preferable.

</details>


### [48] [Clinical Communication Processing with Models Trained on LLM-Generated Synthetic Data: A Structured Survey and Novel Application Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05993)
*Alexander Apartsin,Yehudit Aperstein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大型语言模型生成的合成临床沟通文本（如EMs预到达报告、护士交接、患者门户分诊等）作为训练数据，以引导临床NLP系统的发展，弥补真实临床沟通语料稀缺的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量临床价值蕴含在会话沟通中而非结构化记录中，但真实临床沟通语料因隐私性、碎片化和标注成本高等原因极为稀缺，阻碍了医疗NLP对这类信息的理解与利用。

Method: 采用结构化叙事综述方法，按源表示、沟通形式与参与者、生成方法和下游任务四个维度组织文献，并辅以十三个新颖案例研究，构建无标注真实数据的临床NLP系统。

Result: 合成临床沟通可以有效引导下游NLP系统；微调的编码器模型优于所评估的零样本基线；刻意降级的沟通数据有助于提升系统鲁棒性。局限在于多数研究在合成保留集上评估，而"合成训练-真实测试"的证据仍然有限。

Conclusion: 合成临床沟通正成为一种实用的研究资源，但要将其确立为可复用的临床基础设施，仍需真实数据迁移、安全性和外部验证方面的进一步工作。

Abstract: Much clinical value is conveyed not through structured records but through communication: exchanges in which patients describe symptoms, clinicians reason and give instructions, ambulances hand over to emergency departments, and nurses pass on a shift. Such language differs from tabular data because meaning depends on speaker role, intent, causality, uncertainty, omission, and channel noise. Healthcare natural language processing must therefore interpret information as conveyed rather than coded. This requires well-annotated corpora, which are scarce because authentic exchanges are private, fragmented, and costly to annotate. Large language models offer a way forward by transforming clinical sources, such as records, diagnostic labels, symptom lists, or care plans, into written and transcribed communication for downstream models. We present a structured narrative survey organized by source representation, communication form and participants, generation method, and downstream task, complemented by thirteen novel case studies. These build clinical NLP systems for communication channels and languages without labeled real-world data, including EMS pre-arrival reports, field-radio casualty documentation, nurse handoffs, patient-portal triage, and low-resource discharge communication. They show that synthetic communication can bootstrap such systems. Findings include the competitiveness of fine-tuned encoder models over evaluated zero-shot baselines and the value of deliberately degraded communication for robustness. The main limitation is that most studies evaluate on held-out synthetic communication, while train-on-synthetic, test-on-authentic evidence remains limited. We conclude that syn-thetic clinical communication is becoming a practical research resource; establishing it as reusable clinical infrastructure will require authentic-data transfer, safety and external validation.

</details>


### [49] [EpiBench: Can LLMs Understand Epitopes for Antibody Drug Discovery?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06022)
*Zirui Wang,Jiaqi Wang,Qinghan Wang,Yuzhi Xu,Gang Du,Tingjun Hou,Odin Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出EpiBench，一个基于序列、封闭式、可自动评分的基准测试，用于评估大语言模型在抗体表位推理方面的能力。包含1,609个精选样本，覆盖五个相连任务，评估了九个通用LLM，结果发现当前LLM仅捕获部分表位信号，在抗体特异性序列锚定、长上下文残基定位和生物学推理方面仍有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表位决定抗体的结合位点及下游治疗特性（如功能阻断和逃逸抗性），对抗体药物发现至关重要。尽管LLM展现出强大的生物医学推理能力，但其是否能直接从抗原和抗体序列推断表位信息尚未明确。现有表位资源专注于孤立预测任务或依赖专门的结构设定，缺乏覆盖整个抗体开发流程的表位中心评估基准。

Method: 构建EpiBench基准，包含1,609个基于结构抗体-抗原接触、功能B细胞测定和深度突变扫描逃逸测量所精选的样本。覆盖五个相连任务：可靶向区域发现、抗体条件表位识别、表位分箱、功能表位评估和抗体逃逸评估，并通过受控采样减少捷径评估伪影。评估九个通用LLM，结合任务特化基线、抗原长度分层、显式推理对比和失败模式检查进行分析。

Result: 当前LLM仅捕获部分表位相关信号，但在抗体特异性序列锚定、长上下文残基定位和生物学接地推理方面存在明显局限。任务特化基线和分层分析揭示了LLM在不同表位任务上的能力差异与系统性弱点。

Conclusion: EpiBench为衡量和改进序列感知的生物医学LLM提供了一个诊断性测试平台，为可靠的LLM辅助抗体发现奠定了基础。该基准揭示了现有LLM与可靠表位推理之间的差距，指出了未来需要改进的方向。

Abstract: Epitopes determine where antibodies bind antigens and shape downstream therapeutic properties such as functional blockade and escape resistance, making epitope understanding central to antibody drug discovery. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown strong biomedical reasoning ability, it remains unclear whether they can infer epitope information directly from antigen and antibody sequences. Existing epitope resources typically focus on isolated prediction tasks or rely on specialized structural settings, while general protein benchmarks do not evaluate epitope-centered decisions across the antibody development workflow. To address this gap, we introduce EpiBench, a closed-book, sequence-based, and automatically scorable benchmark for evaluating epitope reasoning in LLMs. EpiBench contains 1,609 curated samples grounded in structural antibody--antigen contacts, curated functional B-cell assays, and deep mutational scanning escape measurements. It covers five connected tasks: targetable region discovery, antibody-conditioned epitope identification, epitope binning, functional epitope assessment, and antibody escape assessment, with controlled sampling to reduce shortcut-based evaluation artifacts. We evaluate nine general-purpose LLMs and analyze their behavior through task-specific baselines, antigen length stratification, explicit-reasoning comparison, and failure-mode inspection. The results show that current LLMs capture partial epitope-related signals but remain limited in antibody-specific sequence grounding, long-context residue localization, and biologically grounded reasoning. Therefore, EpiBench provides a diagnostic testbed for measuring and improving sequence-aware biomedical LLMs toward reliable LLM-assisted antibody discovery.

</details>


### [50] [FormBharo: Designing and Evaluating a Voice Agent for Conversational Form Filling in Rural India](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06027)
*Aman Dalmia,Sanskriti Midha,Jigar Doshi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍FormBharo，一个为印度低识字率人群设计的语音代理系统，通过将大语言模型与确定性规则验证和流程控制相结合，在电话通话中填写结构化表单。系统正在与NGO组织ARMMAN合作试点，并发布了FormVoiceAgentBench基准测试集用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度几乎所有社会福利都始于填表，但最需要这些福利的人往往不识字。目前这项工作由一线卫生工作者逐一为受益人注册，效率低下且浪费有限的资源。需要一种通过语音对话来自动填表的解决方案。

Method: FormBharo将LLM与确定性规则验证及流程控制相结合，在电话通话中填充结构化表单。团队发布了FormVoiceAgentBench基准数据集，包含人工录制的印地语音频和3,760个多轮对话测试（960个模拟通话）。系统采用基于帕累托的加权和标量化方法在准确性、成本和延迟之间选择可部署配置。

Result: 当LLM接收有错误的真实语音转录而非参考转录时，表单完成率下降最多约41个百分点。基于规则的控件恢复了大量回合级提取错误，使较小的廉价模型在表单完成率上达到或超越前沿模型。组件性能不能预测端到端表现：GPT-5.5在参考转录上回合级提取准确率领先（99.8%），但在表单完成率上排名较低。没有单一模型在准确性、成本和延迟上同时最优。

Conclusion: 错误在流水线中既会传播也会抵消，最优模型选择只能通过端到端评估来确定。由于没有单一模型在所有指标上最优，需要采用帕累托最优方法在准确性、成本和延迟之间进行权衡，以选择可部署的配置。

Abstract: In India, almost every social benefit starts with a form, yet the people who need these benefits most are often unable to read or write. Reaching them requires a spoken conversation. Today that work falls to frontline health workers who enroll beneficiaries one at a time, a poor use of stretched capacity. We built FormBharo ("fill the form" in Hindi), a voice agent that fills a structured form over a phone call under tight latency and cost budgets by pairing Large Language Models (LLMs) with deterministic, rule-based validation and flow control. It is being piloted with ARMMAN, an NGO running large-scale maternal and child mobile-health programs in India, to enroll low-income, Hindi-speaking mothers in antenatal and postnatal care. To our knowledge, it is the first voice agent piloted to fill an enrollment form for this population. We openly release FormVoiceAgentBench, a benchmark pairing human-recorded Hindi audio with 3,760 multi-turn conversation tests across 960 simulated calls, to evaluate our agent's components (transcription, extraction, reply generation) and end-to-end form completion under real acoustic variations. Form completion drops by up to ~41 points when LLMs receive error-prone real-speech transcripts instead of reference ones. The rule-based controls recover many turn-level extraction errors, helping smaller, cheaper models match or surpass frontier models on form completion. Component performance does not predict end-to-end performance: GPT-5.5 leads turn-level extraction accuracy on reference transcripts (99.8%) but ranks lower on form completion. Since errors both propagate and cancel across the pipeline, the optimal model choice of models emerges only through end-to-end evaluation. Finally, no single model is best across accuracy, cost, and latency at once, so we use a Pareto-based weighted-sum scalarization to select a deployable configuration balancing the three.

</details>


### [51] [Training-Free Token-Level Steering for LLM Personalized Co-Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06069)
*Wenhao Mao,Chengbin Hou,Weixiao Wang,Jialiang Zhu,Min Liu,Yibin Hao,Hairong Lv*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SteerWrite是一个无需训练的框架，用于个性化协同写作。它无需梯度更新即可将基础模型适配到专业领域和特定数据集，在多个数据集上达到最先进性能，显著减少人工编辑工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽在个性化方面有前景，但缺乏专业领域知识。传统微调方法计算成本高且难以应对快速数据更新，检索增强生成无法提供细粒度的token级引导，且现有系统多局限于聊天界面，协同写作范式在编码领域之外的潜力尚未被充分挖掘。

Method: SteerWrite是一个无需训练（training-free）的框架，通过特定设计适配小数据集场景，无需梯度更新即可将基础模型有效适配到专业领域，实现token级别的个性化协同写作。

Result: SteerWrite在多个数据集、多种评估指标和不同模型上均达到最先进性能，同时显著减少了人工编辑工作。

Conclusion: SteerWrite证明了无需梯度更新的训练免费框架能够在个性化协同写作中有效替代微调和RAG方案，兼顾专业领域适配的同时保持计算效率和实时更新能力。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) show great promise for personalization, they often lack specialized domain knowledge. Conventional solutions like fine-tuning struggle with high computational costs and rapid data updates, while Retrieval-Augmented Generation fails to provide fine-grained, token-level steering. Furthermore, chat-based interfaces remain dominant, whereas productive co-writing paradigms have not yet been well exploited beyond the coding domain. To this end, we introduce SteerWrite, a training-free framework designed for personalized co-writing. Our method effectively adapts the base model to specialized domains without gradient updates, with specific designs tailored to small datasets. Experiments demonstrate that SteerWrite achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse datasets, metrics, and models, significantly reducing human editing effort.

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Sequence Order: Syntax-Informed Positional Embeddings for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06111)
*Haris Riaz,Hyungji Kim,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SiPE（语法信息位置编码），通过在预训练时从依存句法分析中学习轻量级语法先验，并将其注入到三种主流位置编码家族（绝对、相对、旋转）中，实现不修改自注意力机制即可注入句法信息。SiPE在SyntaxGym基准上最高提升10.3%，同时将困惑度降低9.0%，并在GLUE基准上提升最多8.2%，实现了句法监督与推理成本之间的新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer中的位置编码主要编码token距离和顺序，对句法结构基本不敏感。虽然已有多种句法注入方法，但大多在推理时需要边际化多个句法树或运行时分派句法信息，导致推理成本高或句法信息在运行时被丢弃。本文旨在探索一种轻量级、低推理成本的句法注入方式，并系统研究句法先验应注入到模型的位置编码范式。

Method: SiPE从依存句法分析中学习一个轻量级句法先验，在预训练阶段注入到三种主流位置编码家族（绝对、相对、旋转）中，适用于编码器和解码器。研究隔离了先验应进入模型的"位置"和"方式"，发现：对于使用相对PE的自回归解码器，先验与注意力分数中的相对位置项做乘法耦合效果最强；对于编码器，则直接加到输入嵌入上与各编码器原生位置机制组合效果最佳。推理时仅需单一句法树。

Result: 在SyntaxGym基准上最高提升10.3%，同时将困惑度降低9.0%（对比无句法监督的基线模型，而几乎所有现有句法注入方法反而会降低困惑度）。在GLUE基准上提升最高8.2%，证明收益不仅限于句法泛化还能提升真实世界语言理解。与那些推理时需要边际化多个句法树或在运行时分派句法的模型不同，SiPE仅依赖单一解析树。

Conclusion: SiPE证明了轻量级句法先验可通过位置编码有效注入Transformer，且不修改自注意力结构或增加推理成本。其成功取决于架构特性：解码器（相对PE）适合乘法耦合到注意力相对位置项，编码器适合加法注入输入嵌入。SiPE建立了一个句法监督与推理成本之间的新帕累托前沿，同时证明句法注入不仅能提升句法能力，还能改善整体语言建模质量（困惑度与GLUE均受益）。

Abstract: Positional embeddings (PE) in Transformers encode token distance and order but are largely agnostic to \textit{syntactic structure}. We introduce \textbf{S}yntax-\textbf{i}nformed \textbf{P}ositional \textbf{E}mbeddings (\textbf{SiPE}), which learns a lightweight syntactic prior from dependency parses during pretraining and injects it across all three dominant PE families (absolute, relative, rotary), for both encoders and decoders, leaving self-attention and the rest of the architecture untouched. We isolate \emph{where} and \emph{how} the prior should enter the model, and find it depends on the architecture: for autoregressive decoders that use relative PE, the prior is strongest when coupled multiplicatively with the relative-position term of the attention score, outperforming injection into the input embeddings, into self-attention, or into the positional and attention terms jointly---while for encoders it is best added directly to the input embeddings, composing with each encoder's native positional mechanism. We find that models pre-trained with SiPE improve on the SyntaxGym benchmark by up to $10.3\%$ while simultaneously reducing perplexity by $9.0\%$ over a base model with no syntactic supervision---a metric nearly every existing syntax-injection method instead degrades. Crucially, these gains extend beyond syntactic generalization: SiPE also improves real-world language understanding, raising scores on the GLUE benchmark by up to $8.2\%$ over a model trained without it. Unlike existing syntactic language models that marginalize over many parses at inference or discard syntax at runtime, SiPE conditions on a single parse, establishing a new Pareto frontier between syntactic supervision and inference cost.

</details>


### [53] [Decolonizing Linguistic Policies in Automated Speech Recognition: A Framework for Cross-Culturally Competent Speech AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06141)
*Jay L. Cunningham,Mark Atta Mensah,Richard Martinez,Joao Vieira da Silva Neto,Efi Dawodu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨自动语音识别（ASR）系统及其语音界面在公共服务、医疗和教育领域中对低资源、原住民和非标准语言变体的持续性失败问题，主张这些失败不仅是技术缺陷，更是隐含的语言政策，复制了殖民语言等级制度。论文提出"三重危害"（3M）分类法（误识别、错位、不信任）和面向ASR语言多样性的七层情境化模型，并提出了参与式框架和面向文化胜任ASR的最低审计协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR系统对低资源语言、原住民语言和非标准语言变体持续失败，作者认为这不仅是技术层面的错误，更构成了一种隐性的语言政策，在技术系统中复制和维持着殖民时期的语言等级秩序。作者希望从语言学资本、种族语言学意识形态、语言政策研究和去殖民计算等交叉视角揭示其深层机制。

Method: 论文采用跨学科的理论分析方法，融合语言学资本理论、种族语言学意识形态、语言政策研究和去殖民计算等框架，提出"三重危害"（3M）分类法——误识别（Misrecognition）、错位（Misalignment）和不信任（Mistrust），并构建了一个七层情境化模型来分析ASR及ASR中介语音界面中的语言多样性问题。

Result: 提出了"三重危害"（3M）分类法和七层情境化模型，作为系统理解ASR中语言多样化问题（涉及数据、指标和模型先验如何决定哪些人的声音可被机器读取）的分析工具；同时提出了一个参与式框架和最低审计协议，将受影响的社区定位为共同设计者、评估者和治理伙伴。

Conclusion: ASR系统中的语言多样性问题需要从"技术修复"转向"政治与伦理修复"：必须将受影响社区纳入ASR系统的设计、评估和治理全过程，通过参与式框架和文化胜任的审计机制，实现更具公平性和去殖民性的语音技术生态。

Abstract: This paper focuses on automatic speech recognition (ASR) and ASR-mediated voice interfaces that shape access to public services, healthcare, and education. We argue that persistent failures for low-resource, Indigenous, and non-standard language varieties are not only technical errors, but also implicit linguistic policies that reproduce colonial language hierarchies. Drawing on linguistic capital, raciolinguistic ideology, language policy research, and decolonial computing, we show how data, metrics, and model priors determine whose voices become machine-legible. We introduce the Three Harms (3M) taxonomy---Misrecognition, Misalignment, and Mistrust---and a seven-layer situatedness model for linguistic diversity in ASR and ASR-mediated voice interfaces. We then propose a participatory framework and minimum audit protocol for culturally competent ASR, positioning affected communities as co-designers, evaluators, and governance partners.

</details>


### [54] [Routing Is Least Learnable Where It Is Most Valuable: Bounds on Representation Routing for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06171)
*Jiaming Wei,Zekun Wu,Adriano Koshiyama,Maria Perez-Ortiz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了Web代理在文本、像素或两者兼有的观测模式下，针对不同任务动态选择观测模式（路由）的价值。在VisualWebArena和WebArena的8个站点-模型组合上测量了6种观测模式，发现模式互补且最优选择在任务集间反转。表面上看oracle路由的收益巨大，但被12-14%的运行噪声所膨胀。通过成本约束过滤无人能解的任务可降低9.5-30.6%成本。测试了5种路由策略，均无法稳健胜过固定选择一个好的模式，根本障碍是路由监督信号受限于代理成功率——代理越弱，路由可获得的标签越少，而这恰恰是路由最有价值之处。但这一限制属于当前代理而非路由本身，因为标签供给与路由机会高度相关（相关系数0.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web代理的观测模式（文本、像素或两者）通常一旦选定就固定应用于所有任务，但不同任务可能适合不同观测模式。本文旨在量化按任务动态选择观测模式（即路由）能带来多大收益，并探究其可行性与根本障碍。

Method: 在VisualWebArena和WebArena上，对8个站点-模型组合（cells）测量6种观测模式的表现。通过重跑模式测量运行噪声（12-14%结果变化）。构建oracle上限基准，提出成本约束（只将无模式可解的任务发送给最便宜模式）。测试5种路由策略：模式选择、强模式支出决策、基于任务文本的零成本规则、置信度级联、成本分层池化。

Result: 6种观测模式互补，每种都能解决其他模式遗漏的任务，失败方式在结构上不同，最佳模式选择在任务集间反转。oracle路由看似收益巨大但被运行噪声膨胀；重跑已有模式获得的增益与新加模式相当。成本约束在8/8的cell中以不降低成功率的方式削减9.5-30.6%成本。5种路由策略均不能稳健击败固定选择一个好的模式，唯一例外是在最稀疏cell中的脆弱结果。路由监督由代理成功率产生，代理越弱路由器得到的标签越少，恰恰在路由最有价值处受限。

Conclusion: 当前路由无法稳健胜过固定模式选择，根本障碍在于路由监督信号依赖代理自身成功率——弱代理恰恰无法产生足够标签来训练有用路由。但该限制属于当前代理水平而非路由范式本身：代理越强，标签供给与路由机会同步上升（相关系数0.95），因此更强的代理可以扭转这一结论。论文提供了完整的测量协议与噪声带以供后续复现。

Abstract: Web agents observe a browser through text, pixels, or both, and the choice is usually fixed once for all tasks. We measure six observation modes across eight site-model combinations (cells) on VisualWebArena and WebArena and ask what choosing per task would buy. The modes are complementary: each solves tasks the others miss, they fail in structurally different ways, and the best choice reverses between task sets. The obvious prize, an oracle that picks a winning mode for every task, looks large but is inflated by run-to-run noise: rerunning the same mode on the same tasks changes 12-14% of outcomes, so a second run of a mode already in hand gains about as much as adding a new one. What survives is a cost bound: sending only the tasks no mode solves to the cheapest mode cuts cost by 9.5-30.6% in 8 of 8 cells at unchanged success. We then test five routing policies (picking the mode, deciding when to spend on the strong mode, a zero-cost rule read off the task text, a confidence cascade, and pooled cost tiers), and none robustly beats simply fixing one well-chosen mode; the one exception is a fragile result in our sparsest cell. The central obstruction is that routing supervision is produced at the agent's success rate: the weaker the agent, the fewer labels a router gets, exactly where routing would be most valuable. This limit belongs to today's agents rather than to routing itself. Label supply and routing opportunity rise together (correlation 0.95 across cells), so a stronger agent can overturn the result, and we report the rerun noise bands and the full measurement protocol.

</details>


### [55] [NeSy-RAG: Neuro-Symbolic RAG for Explainable Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06292)
*Jonas Gann,Michael Gertz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NeSy-RAG是一个模块化的神经符号RAG框架，它从检索到的文本块中合成可溯源的Prolog模块，将自然语言转化为可执行的符号推理查询，并通过符号知识缺口检测机制自动识别和询问缺失的用户上下文。在ShARC基准上，无需领域特定训练即可达到61.1%的准确率，远超同模型RAG基线（42.8%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RAG系统的推理过程不透明，中间推理步骤难以验证且无法可靠追溯到具体证据；同时，缺失的用户特定上下文很少被系统性地检测到，经常导致不完整或错误的输出。

Method: NeSy-RAG由三个关键组件构成：(1) 从每个检索到的文本块合成语义谓词编码布尔声明（可依赖用户事实）的Prolog模块；(2) 利用联合自然语言-代码嵌入进行谓词检索和组合，形成Prolog查询；(3) 提出符号知识缺口检测机制，识别影响查询结果的缺失用户事实，并自动触发后续交互。

Result: 在ShARC基准上，无需领域特定训练，NeSy-RAG达到61.1%的准确率，比同模型RAG基线（42.8%）高出18.3个百分点。

Conclusion: NeSy-RAG通过神经符号推理实现了可溯源的、确定性的RAG回答生成，同时能主动检测和补救缺失的用户上下文，在显著提升准确率的同时保证了推理过程的透明性和可信度。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves question answering by grounding large language models (LLMs) in external knowledge such as text corpora. However, its reasoning process remains largely opaque: intermediate reasoning steps are difficult to verify and cannot be reliably attributed to specific evidence. Moreover, missing user-specific context is rarely detected systematically, often leading to incomplete or incorrect output.
  We propose NeSy-RAG, a modular neuro-symbolic RAG framework that synthesizes attributable Prolog modules from retrieved text chunks. For each chunk, the system generates semantically meaningful predicates that encode Boolean claims, which may depend on user facts. Using joint natural language-code embeddings, predicates are retrieved and composed into Prolog queries. To address incomplete user context, we introduce a symbolic knowledge-gap detection mechanism that identifies missing user facts whose truth values affect the query outcome and automatically triggers follow-up interactions.
  Executing the resulting Prolog queries yields deterministic answers together with transparent execution traces that link each reasoning step to its originating source. On the ShARC benchmark, without domain-specific training, NeSy-RAG achieves 61.1% accuracy, outperforming a same-model RAG baseline that achieves 42.8% accuracy.

</details>


### [56] [Benchmarking and Enhancing LLMs for Rule-Intensive Review of National Standard Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06312)
*Tao Wang,Qihao Yang,Rongjiao Liang,Lianghong Lin,Haitao Wang,Xinyu Cao,Tianyong Hao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GB/T-Bench，首个面向国家标准文档（如中国GB/T标准）结构化审查的基准测试，包含层级式审查分类体系（5个维度、25种可诊断错误类型）、可控反例生成机制（处理488份文档生成7,306个可追溯审查错误实例）、诊断导向的评估协议，以及多智能体框架GB/T-Reviewer。实验表明最强LLM仅达0.3280 CMCS（专家为0.6640），而GB/T-Reviewer将最佳CMCS提升至0.5094，揭示了人机差距并验证了结构化技能协同在规则密集型文档审查中的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在复杂专业任务中应用日益广泛，但规则密集型文档审查能力尚未得到充分评估。现有基准聚焦领域知识与问答，忽视了对专业文档的内在质量审查。国家标准文档（如GB/T标准）篇幅长、结构高度规范、受明确规则约束，是理想的测试平台，但此类审查严重依赖人类专家，成本高且难以规模化。

Method: 该方法包含三部分：(1) GB/T Review Taxonomy，一个层级式审查分类体系，涵盖文档结构、范围一致性、规范性情态、术语一致性、规范性引用5个维度，共25种可诊断错误类型；(2) 可控反例生成机制，结合确定性规则与受限LLM重写，将488份文档处理为7,306个可追溯的审查错误实例；(3) GB/T-Reviewer多智能体框架，将审查知识转化为专门技能，协调全局检查、定向诊断、规则扫描和结果验证四个模块。评估协议要求对错误位置、审查维度和错误类型进行精确匹配，并计算文档级覆盖率指标（CMCS）。

Result: 对14个主流LLM的实验揭示了显著的人机差距：最强模型仅达到0.3280 CMCS，而人类专家为0.6640。GB/T-Reviewer框架将最佳CMCS提升至0.5094，验证了结构化技能协同在规则密集型文档审查中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作为标准化领域及其他高利害文档领域中的可信AI铺平了道路，证明了通过结构化技能协同可以显著提升LLM在规则密集型文档审查任务上的表现，但仍存在可观的人机差距，未来仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly support complex professional tasks, yet their capabilities in rule-intensive document review remain insufficiently evaluated. National standard documents, such as China GB/T standards, offer a representative testbed: they are lengthy, highly structured, and governed by explicit rules for scope, terminology, normative wording, and cross-section consistency. Existing benchmarks focus on domain knowledge and question answering, largely overlooking intrinsic quality review for professional documents. Such reviews rely heavily on human experts, making them costly and difficult to scale. To bridge this gap, we introduce GB/T-Bench, the first benchmark for the structured review of national standard documents. Its GB/T Review Taxonomy is a hierarchical schema covering document structure, scope alignment, normative modality, terminology consistency, and normative references, with 25 diagnosable error types. A controllable counterexample generation mechanism combines deterministic rules and constrained LLM rewriting to process 488 documents into 7,306 traceable review error instances for evaluation. We also develop a diagnosis-oriented evaluation protocol requiring exact matches on error location, review dimension, and error type, plus document-level coverage metrics. We further propose GB/T-Reviewer, a multi-agent framework that converts review knowledge into specialized skills and coordinates global inspection, targeted diagnosis, rule scanning, and result verification. Experiments with 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a substantial human-LLM gap: the strongest model achieves only 0.3280 CMCS versus 0.6640 for experts. GB/T-Reviewer raises the best CMCS to 0.5094, showing the value of structured skill coordination for rule-intensive document review. This work paves the way for trustworthy AI in standardization and other high-stakes document domains.

</details>


### [57] [Benchmarking the Benchmarks: Evaluating Benchmarks for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06329)
*Noam Koren,Roy Bar-Haim,Abigail Goldsteen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个无参考框架，利用LLM评审员评估任务型对话agent基准测试的一致性、复杂性和策略覆盖率，并提供可操作的弱点诊断，通过人工标注和多种扰动实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任务型对话agent常通过精选或自动生成的基准进行评估，但基准质量很少被评估。低质量基准可能包含不一致的任务、过于简单的场景或有限的策略覆盖，导致评估结果不可靠。

Method: 提出一个无需参考标注的框架，使用LLM作为评审员来评估基准的三方面质量：一致性、复杂性和策略覆盖率，同时提供针对弱点的可操作诊断。通过人工标注验证一致性，并通过评估不同能力LLM生成的基准及受控质量退化扰动后的基准来验证框架有效性。

Result: 在多个领域和不同的评审模型中，提出的指标能够一致地区分不同质量的基准。该框架还适用于人工精选的基准，展示了广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: 该框架为评估合成和人工精选的对话agent基准提供了一种实用方法，有助于确保基准质量，从而提高对话agent评估的可靠性。

Abstract: Task-oriented conversational agents are evaluated using curated or automatically generated benchmarks, yet benchmark quality is rarely assessed. Poor benchmarks may contain inconsistent tasks, simplistic scenarios, or limited policy coverage, leading to unreliable evaluations. We introduce a reference-free framework that uses LLM judges to assess benchmark consistency, complexity, and policy coverage, while providing actionable diagnostics of weaknesses. We validate the framework by demonstrating agreement with independent human annotations and by evaluating benchmarks generated by LLMs of varying capabilities, as well as benchmarks subjected to controlled quality-degrading perturbations. Across domains and judge models, the proposed metrics consistently distinguish between benchmark quality levels. We further demonstrate the framework's applicability to manually curated benchmarks. Our framework offers a practical approach for evaluating synthetic and manually curated conversational-agent benchmarks.

</details>


### [58] [RP-OPSD: Reasoning-Pivot-Guided On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06347)
*Xinye Wang,Junxiao Liu,Shujian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RP-OPSD（推理枢轴引导的在线策略自蒸馏）方法，通过识别目标语言推理中的"推理枢轴"（推动或重定向推理过程的关键决策），并利用有无英文参考解时教师视图间的分布偏移作为操作代理，对这类关键token进行特权蒸馏和参考锚定，从而提升多语言推理迁移能力。在17种语言、多难度级别的数学推理基准上超越现有强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言推理迁移对将LLM的推理能力扩展到高资源语言之外至关重要。OPSD及其变体虽提供密集token级监督，但其目标并未显式优先考虑对跨语言迁移最关键的推理信号。作者将目标语言推理分为表面文本生成和推理枢轴（推进或重定向推理过程的决策），认为需要将特权蒸馏集中在这些枢轴上。

Method: 提出RP-OPSD（Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation）。核心思想是：使用在有无英文参考解条件下的匹配教师视图之间的分布差异作为操作代理，来识别推理枢轴，并据此引导特权蒸馏与参考锚定。即利用分布偏移程度来定位那些真正驱动推理进程的关键token，将密集蒸馏资源集中于其上。

Result: 在覆盖17种语言、多难度级别的数学推理基准上，RP-OPSD优于强多语言推理基线和OPSD变体。进一步分析显示，该方法将特权蒸馏集中于推理控制token和问题条件化的状态更新token，而对主要支撑表面实现的token降低权重。

Conclusion: 通过推理枢轴引导的特权蒸馏能够更有效地实现跨语言推理迁移，识别并聚焦于推理的关键决策点而非表面语言实现，是多语言推理增强的有效途径。

Abstract: Multilingual reasoning transfer is crucial for extending reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs) beyond high-resource languages. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) and its variants have emerged as a promising paradigm, providing dense token-level supervision on student-generated rollouts, yet their objectives do not explicitly prioritize reasoning signals most critical to cross-lingual transfer. We characterize that target-language reasoning comprises the generation of both surface text and reasoning pivots, which are decisions that advance or redirect the reasoning process and shape subsequent inference. This motivates concentrating privileged distillation around such pivots. We therefore propose RP-OPSD, Reasoning-Pivot-guided On-Policy Self-Distillation, using the distributional shift between matched teacher views with and without an English reference solution as an operational proxy to guide privileged distillation and reference anchoring. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks covering 17 languages and multiple difficulty levels show that our method outperforms strong multilingual reasoning baselines and OPSD variants. Further analysis reveals that RP-OPSD concentrates privileged distillation on reasoning-control and problem-condistioned state-update tokens, while downweighting it for tokens that mainly support surface realization. Our code is available at https://github.com/NJUNLP/RP-OPSD.

</details>


### [59] [The Bitter Lesson of Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06370)
*Ishan Patel,Sahil Sen,Elias Lumer,Vamse Kumar Subbiah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统比较了程序化工具调用（PTC）与原生JSON工具调用在BFCL v4基准上的表现，覆盖14个语言模型。结果显示PTC在11/14个模型中匹配或超越JSON工具调用，GPT-5.6系列提升10.6%，在并行扇出场景13/14模型占优，且在上下文腐化场景中表现更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然程序化工具调用（将工具暴露为Python类型存根，通过代码调用）被认为比JSON工具调用更具灵活性和扩展性，但缺乏在既有基准上跨当前及前代模型、真实任务条件下的系统性评测，以验证其实际优越性和稳健性。

Method: 在BFCL v4基准上，对14个语言模型分别进行程序化工具调用（PTC）与原生JSON工具调用两种范式的经验对比实验。PTC范式下，工具以类型化Python存根形式暴露，模型通过代码调用，执行与结果处理在单一agent回合内完成。实验覆盖整体性能、并行扇出场景及上下文腐化（context rot）条件下的稳定性。

Result: PTC在BFCL v4上11/14个模型匹配或超过JSON工具调用；GPT-5.6系列比JSON基线提升10.6%；并行扇出场景下13/14个模型匹配或优于基线；在上下文腐化条件下保持稳定，而基线平均退化2.3%。

Conclusion: 程序化工具调用是JSON工具调用的可行且稳健的替代方案，其性能与模型发布代际的能力演化保持一致，具有跨模型代际的通用适用性。

Abstract: Tool use transforms LLMs into agents that act beyond their training data, and for code-capable models, programmatic tool calling extends this further by replacing rigid JSON calls with scripts that chain and parallelize naturally. However, a systematic evaluation of tools as code on an established benchmark across current and prior model generations under real-world task conditions has not been conducted. In this work, we empirically compare programmatic tool calling (PTC) to native JSON tool calling across 14 language models on BFCL v4. In the programmatic tool calling paradigm, tools are exposed as typed Python stubs that the model invokes through code, with execution and results handled in a single agent turn. Programmatic tool calling matches or exceeds native JSON tool calling in 11 of 14 models on BFCL v4, with the GPT-5.6 family achieving a 10.6% improvement over the JSON tool calling baseline. Further, it matches or outperforms baseline in 13 of 14 models under parallel fan-out, and holds stable under context rot conditions where baseline degrades 2.3% on average. Our results demonstrate that programmatic tool calling is a viable and robust alternative to JSON tool calling, with performance tracking model capability across release generations.

</details>


### [60] [Learning When to Trust via Selective Context Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06377)
*Xian Sun,Wei Chow,Yingshuo Wang,Junhao Liu,Wei Gao,Qing Wu,Lingdong Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文重新定义语言模型对外部信号的鲁棒性问题为"选择性信任"问题。作者提出MIST基准（人工标注，四种匹配条件：干净、误导、正确上下文、无关上下文）和SC2W配对指标（衡量误导信号将正确答案翻转为错误的频率），发现误导信号的易受影响性是普遍存在的。随后提出SCOPE方法，通过挖掘干净-正确/误导-错误的失败案例，并在四种条件下均衡平衡的配对偏好对上优化DPO目标，大幅降低SC2W同时保持干净/正确/无关上下文下的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法只关注让模型抵抗误导信号，可能陷入失败模式：完全忽略上下文的模型看似鲁棒，但在上下文值得信任时却毫无用处。因此需要将问题重新定义为选择性信任——模型应当既能抵抗误导信号，又能利用有价值的上下文。

Method: 1) 构建MIST基准：每个推理题在四种匹配条件下呈现（干净、误导、正确上下文、无关上下文）；2) 提出SC2W配对指标：计算误导信号将干净-正确答案翻转为错误的频率；3) 提出SCOPE方法：挖掘干净-正确/误导-错误的失败对，在四种条件均衡平衡的配对偏好对上优化标准DPO目标（而非仅用误导项）。

Result: 在全面的基准研究中发现误导信号的易受影响性是普遍的。SCOPE在流行的开源模型上显著降低了SC2W，同时保持了在干净、正确和无关上下文条件下的准确率。

Conclusion: 模型评价应当基于选择性信任而非单纯的对误导信号的抵抗能力。SCOPE证明了一种平衡的多条件偏好优化方法可以同时实现抗误导和善用上下文的目标。

Abstract: Language models increasingly condition their answers on external signals, and a single misleading one can turn a correct answer wrong. The obvious remedy, training models to resist such signals, hides a failure mode: a model that ignores all context looks robust yet is useless when the context is worth trusting. We recast the problem as selective trust and introduce MIST, a human-annotated benchmark that renders each reasoning item under four matched conditions (clean, misleading, correct-context, and irrelevant-context), together with SC2W, a paired metric counting how often a misleading signal flips a clean-correct answer to wrong. Across a comprehensive benchmark study, we observe that such a susceptibility is universal. We then propose SCOPE, which mines clean-correct/misleading-wrong failures and optimizes a standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) objective over matched preference pairs balanced equally across all four conditions, rather than over misleading items alone. Our approach substantially reduces SC2W on popular open-sourced models while preserving accuracy when the added context is clean, correct, or irrelevant. With this work, we argue that models should be judged on selective trust, not on resistance alone.

</details>

### [61] [Contour integral methods and model order reduction for parametric linear control systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05363)
*Serkan Gugercin,Mattia Manucci*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轮廓积分方法（CIM），用于在指定时间区间内以用户要求的精度高效计算参数化线性控制系统（控制形式）的输出。该方法通过沿修正积分轮廓应用求积规则来近似逆拉普拉斯变换，并与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）技术相结合，其中贪心算法根据推导的误差估计构建投影空间，且该框架自然满足Hermite插值条件，从而显著降低跨参数域评估输入输出关系的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对参数化线性控制系统，在指定时间区间内以用户规定精度高效计算输出是一个具有挑战性的问题。传统方法在参数域上评估输入输出关系时计算成本高昂，需要一种既能保证精度又能显著降低计算代价的方法。

Method: 1) 采用轮廓积分法（CIM）通过沿修正积分轮廓的求积规则近似逆拉普拉斯变换；2) 将CIM与基于投影的模型降阶（MOR）集成，利用贪心算法构建投影空间；3) 为此设置推导误差估计用于指导贪心算法；4) 证明所提出的投影框架自然满足Hermite插值条件。

Result: 在基准的非参数化和参数化控制系统中验证了该方法的精度和效率，并与最先进的基于投影的MOR方法进行了比较，展示了在广泛输入函数类型和在低维子空间良好捕获的初始条件下的显著性能优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的CIM与基于投影的MOR结合方法，通过贪心算法和误差估计的指导以及Hermite插值条件的自然满足，能够在大幅降低计算成本的同时保持精度，为参数化线性控制系统的输出评估提供了一种高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a contour integral method (CIM) for efficiently computing outputs of parametric linear systems in control form over specified time intervals and to a user-prescribed accuracy. The CIM approximates the inverse Laplace transform via a quadrature rule applied along a modified integration contour. For parametric systems, we show how CIM integrates effectively with projection-based model order reduction (MOR) where a greedy algorithm builds the projection spaces following an error estimate we derive for this setting. We additionally demonstrate that the developed projection framework naturally enforces Hermite interpolation conditions. This combination substantially lowers the cost of evaluating the input-output relations across the parameter domain, for a wide range of input functions, and for initial conditions well captured by a low-dimensional subspace.. We demonstrate the accuracy and efficiency of the approach on benchmark non-parametric and parametric control systems, comparing against state-of-the-art projection-based MOR methods.

</details>


### [62] [An Exact Solution of the Two-Ball Multi-Look Search Game with Three Boxes and Heterogeneous Costs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05406)
*Igor Kleiner*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文精确求解了一个异构多查询搜索成本博弈：藏匿者将两个相同球分布到三个搜索成本满足a≥b≥c>0的盒子中，搜索者自适应开盒直至找到两球。博弈值由三个显式有理函数的最大值给出，对应的最优藏匿策略为乘积分布。所有非浪费确定性搜索者策略等价于72个基本决策树，仅产生42种不同的开盒次数分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决异构成本下多查询搜索博弈的精确解。虽然均匀成本情形已有成熟理论，但异构搜索成本使问题复杂度显著提升，此前缺乏一个完整的精确解析刻画，而非仅数值或渐近结果。

Method: 通过枚举和化简将所有非浪费确定性搜索者策略分为72个基本决策树（仅42种开盒次数分布）。将博弈转化为零和线性规划，分别在三个参数区域构造最优藏匿策略（乘积分布分别限制在三种、五种或全部六种放置形式上），并用精确有理 Bernstein 基证书验证参数化混合策略在全成本区域的可行性。

Result: 博弈值等于三个显式有理函数的最大值；找到了三个参数区域的最优 Hider 策略以及对应的 Searcher 混合策略（第二区域需两种精确混合，第三区域需四种）。独立实现复现了策略集、零和线性规划值以及 Bernstein 验证器检查的所有154个节点。

Conclusion: 本文为异构多查询搜索成本博弈提供了完整的精确解析解，并辅以计算验证，填补了该方向的理论空白。

Abstract: A Hider distributes two identical balls among three boxes whose search costs satisfy $a\ge b\ge c>0$. A Searcher opens boxes adaptively until both balls are found; every opening incurs the corresponding box cost and recovers at most one ball. We give an exact solution of this heterogeneous multi-look search-cost game. The value is the maximum of three explicit rational functions. In the three parameter regimes, an optimal Hider strategy is the product-form distribution restricted respectively to three, five, or all six placements. Every non-wasteful deterministic Searcher policy is payoff-equivalent to one of $72$ elementary decision trees, which induce only $42$ distinct opening-count profiles. An analytic argument settles the first regime. Two exact Searcher mixtures settle the second, and four settle the third. Feasibility of the parameterized mixtures over the full cost region is established by exact rational Bernstein-basis certificates. Independent implementations reproduce the policy set, the zero-sum linear-program values, and all $154$ dyadic nodes examined by the Bernstein verifier.

</details>


### [63] [A Relaxation-Based Decomposition Approach for Solving a Supported-Evacuation Problem in Wildfires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05413)
*Shahryar Moradi,Antoine Sauré,Jonathan Patrick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对野火场景下弱势群体（如医院患者和长期护理居民）的应急疏散问题，提出了一种两阶段随机优化模型，同时优化设施选址、车队规模和车辆路径。采用基于逻辑的Benders分解方法（结合组合Benders割与逻辑不等式）来处理NP难问题，并通过科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火期间弱势群体的受援疏散（如医院患者和长期护理居民）是一个关键但尚未充分研究的问题，需要系统性的优化方法来提升疏散效率。

Method: 构建两阶段随机优化模型，联合优化设施选址、车队规模与车辆路径，并施加严格时间窗约束；针对NP难复杂度，提出基于逻辑的Benders分解（Logic Based Benders Decomposition），引入组合Benders割（Combinatorial Benders Cuts）和基于逻辑的不等式以加速求解。

Result: 大量数值实验及科罗拉多州Roxborough Park社区野火演练的真实数据表明，所提方法能获得高质量解，在庇护所选址、车辆利用率及整体疏散效率方面均显著优于替代策略。

Conclusion: 所提出的随机优化框架与分解求解方法可有效提升弱势群体在野火场景下的疏散效率，为应急管理部门提供科学的决策支持工具。

Abstract: This study addresses the critical yet under researched area of supported evacuation for vulnerable populations during wildfires, such as hospital patients and long term care residents, by developing a two stage stochastic optimization model that optimizes facility location, fleet sizing, and vehicle routing under strict time windows. To overcome the problem NP hard complexity, the authors propose an innovative solution methodology leveraging Logic Based Benders Decomposition, featuring Combinatorial Benders Cuts and logic based inequalities. Extensive numerical experiments and real world data from a community wildfire drill in Roxborough Park, Colorado, demonstrate that the proposed approach yields high quality solutions, significantly improving shelter placement, vehicle utilization, and overall evacuation efficiency compared to alternative policies.

</details>


### [64] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化"期望代价（被积函数可能非光滑非凸）+ 下半连续近端有界函数"之和的问题。该方法将期望代价替换为逐步精化的样本平均，并通过Armijo型线搜索在随机误差下确保充分下降性质。在仅要求样本量非减且无界的宽松条件下，建立了函数值几乎必然收敛和驻点的子序列收敛；借助KL性质升级为整条轨迹收敛，并在指数型KL去奇异函数和多项式增长样本量下推导出多项式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机优化方法在处理非光滑非凸目标、非（弱）凸正则化项以及随机预言机方差无界等情况时存在局限。作者旨在提出一个更通用的框架，放宽对正则化函数凸性及随机方差有界性的假设，并希望获得即使在光滑情形下也是全新的收敛性保证。

Method: 所提方法的关键要素包括：(1) 被积函数满足决策变量方向上非光滑局部化的下降引理变体（涵盖Lipschitz梯度光滑损失及此类损失与凸函数之差）；(2) 每次迭代以逐步精化的样本平均替换期望代价；(3) 采用Armijo型线搜索选择近端次梯度步长，在样本近似引起的随机误差下维持充分下降性质；(4) 收敛性分析仅需样本量序列非减且无界，不规定具体增长率；(5) 利用Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz（KL）性质将子序列收敛升级为整条轨迹收敛，并针对指数型KL函数和多项式增长样本量推导显式多项式收敛速率（含对数因子）。

Result: 建立了函数值序列几乎必然收敛以及轨迹每个聚点均为驻点的结论，且该保证在光滑情形下也是新的。利用KL性质，收敛性可强化为整条轨迹收敛到单一驻点。对指数型KL去奇异函数和多项式增长样本量，获得了函数值与迭代点序列的多项式收敛速率（至多一个对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该框架比现有方法支持更一般的问题形式：既不需要正则化的（弱）凸性，也不需要随机预言机方差的统一有界性。收敛性保证即使在光滑设定下也具有新颖性，并涵盖从几乎必然收敛到KL性质下的全域收敛及显式速率估计。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>

Method: RD-YYF的核心分为离线与在线两个阶段。离线阶段：1) 利用PINNs生成FKE解的若干快照；2) 通过主成分分析（PCA）对密度演化进行低维建模；3) 构建轻量残差代理网络，将初始条件系数与域中心映射到终端解系数。在线阶段：在最近状态估计为中心的固定尺寸窗口内，由预训练代理预测密度演化，随后执行观测更新与状态估计。

Conclusion: RD-YYF通过域重定心策略将计算资源集中在后验密度主导区域，结合PINN与PCA的低维代理建模，在保证跟踪精度的同时实现了高效的非线性目标跟踪，展示了其在实际非线性跟踪场景中的潜力。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation. Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

Method: 实现了一个开源软件包，核心算法为Benders型分解，包含标准割族（Benders、强化Benders、Lagrangian割）以及用于保证一般混合整数状态变量收敛性的ReLU割。针对对偶多最优解问题，提出两种割选择策略：基于对偶问题归一化的策略和基于正则化的策略。此外还引入交替割准则，根据场景自动选择廉价Benders割或昂贵紧割。

Result: 在四类多阶段随机整数规划基准问题上进行了计算实验，评估了所提方法的性能，并揭示了问题结构对实际性能的影响规律。

Conclusion: 通过归一化和正则化两种割选择策略以及交替割准则，有效提升了ReLU割的逼近质量与算法整体效率；实验结果展示了不同问题结构下各策略的适用性，为多阶段随机整数规划的实用化求解提供了开源工具与实验依据。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

Conclusion: 联合学习感知成本和决策规则的逆优化框架能更灵活、更准确地捕捉和复制专家决策过程，在各种工程应用场景中具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>

*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在Bregman损失下拟合线性回归模型时目标函数可能非凸的问题。作者将Hessian表示为带残差依赖权重的加权Gram矩阵，据此导出局部强凸性、光滑性和梯度下降条件线性收敛的充分条件；针对二次-四次势函数推导了精确的标量凸性条件，并通过数值实验表明标量条件在实际中可能过于保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的Bregman回归假设生成势函数φ为强凸时目标函数就是凸的，但本文指出在实际中即使φ强凸，由于损失函数中二阶项引入的负曲率，回归目标关于θ可能非凸。这一凸性差距给优化理论分析带来困难，需要刻画残差依赖曲率如何与生成势函数及设计矩阵相互作用。

Method: 本文将损失函数的Hessian表示为加权Gram矩阵（权重依赖于势函数的导数与当前残差），利用该表示推导局部强凸性、光滑性以及梯度下降条件线性收敛的条件。针对二次-四次势函数φ(t)=t²+t⁴/2，推导出精确的标量凸性条件，识别负曲率区间，并给出曲率正定的局部与全局充分条件。

Result: 导出了Hessian的加权Gram矩阵表示及相应局部凸性、光滑性和收敛性条件；对二次-四次势函数获得精确的标量凸性条件与负曲率区间刻画。数值实验表明：标量条件在Hessian实际正定时可能仍不满足（即标量条件过于保守）；梯度下降的收敛步长范围随四次项参数增大而缩小。

Conclusion: 文章系统刻画了反向Bregman回归中残差依赖曲率与生成势函数、设计矩阵之间的相互作用。核心洞见是：即使生成势函数强凸，Bregman损失下的回归目标仍可能非凸，且其优化几何由残差敏感的权重决定；解析标量条件虽简洁但数值上偏保守，需要更精确的矩阵级分析来理解实际收敛行为。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [73] [A Damped Subspace Splitting Algorithm for Constrained Density Functional Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05682)
*Yuanming Su,Yukuan Hu,Xin Liu,Guanghui Hu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出DASSP算法，一种基于子空间分裂重构和单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法（ADMM）的约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）求解算法，首次为CDFT计算提供严格收敛保证，并在合成与实际问题中展示高可行精度和高效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT求解涉及Stiefel流形约束与非凸二次约束，离散化优化问题极具挑战。现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双循环迭代结构面临收敛困难，亟需一种既能精确满足约束又具有收敛保证的高效算法。

Method: 首先利用旋转不变性引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂（subspace-splitting）重构，将两组约束解耦；基于该重构提出单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法DASSP。每步迭代包含谱最小化步、投影梯度步和阻尼对偶上升步，均可高效实现。

Result: 在合成和实际CDFT问题上的数值结果表明，DASSP达到了较高的可行精确度，展现出良好的效率且不牺牲鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DASSP是首个具备严格收敛保证的CDFT优化算法，有望为可靠高效的大规模CDFT应用铺平道路。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [74] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了图的度序列优化问题，即在一个给定图中寻找一个子图，使得所有顶点上给定函数在子图度数处取值之和最大化。重点分析了二次函数情形下的复杂度，引入了图的临界根概念，并证明了最优值作为二次根的函数是凸分段仿射的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题是一个基础且重要的组合优化问题，理解其在不同函数类型下的计算复杂度对理论研究和实际应用都有价值。特别是二次函数情形下，最优值的行为特征尚未被充分理解。

Method: 引入图的临界根（critical roots）概念来刻画问题的结构特性。通过临界根将二次参数的范围划分为若干区间，并分析在这些区间内最优值函数的变化性质。

Result: 证明了当二次根变化时，问题的最优值函数是凸的分段仿射函数，且各段由图的临界根界定。

Conclusion: 图的临界根为理解二次度序列优化问题的最优值结构提供了新的刻画工具，揭示了最优值函数的凸分段仿射特性，为后续复杂度分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>

*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*
