Motivation: 空中传感器网络在安全敏感场景（如机场监控）中部署时面临传感器位置不确定性的挑战，且不同区域的安全优先级不同，现有方法未能同时兼顾鲁棒性和优先级加权覆盖的优化需求。

Method: 构建包含监控约束和基于RRF（鲁棒正则化公式）鲁棒性建模的数学优化模型，提出优先级感知鲁棒朝向优化（PAROO）算法，用于确定传感器朝向以最大化加权覆盖目标。

Result: 在机场启发式监控场景的实验中，该框架有效将感知资源导向高优先级区域，相比代表性基线方法实现了更高的加权覆盖率，验证了其在安全敏感环境中的实用性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在传感器位置不确定条件下有效权衡覆盖质量与鲁棒性，同时优先保障高优先级区域的覆盖，具有实际安全监控应用价值。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [76] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为双层防御-攻击者模型（BDAM）提供精确最优解基准，通过下层级对偶化将问题重构为单层混合整数规划（MILP），在18个中小规模算例上认证最优性，并指出原启发式方法的解平均低于最优前沿3.55%；同时揭示供给规则在平局情形下的病态性（平局移动造成供给成本偏差高达0.83单位），最后提出MILP-DA混合算法，在17/18个认证算例和全部18个大规模算例上优于已发表的启发式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层互通模型（bi-level interdiction model）通常采用元启发式求解，但在所测试的规模上缺乏精确最优解，导致解质量无法评估。作者此前用混合元启发式求解BDAM模型，本研究旨在为其提供精确最优解作为真实基准，以量化启发式方法的实际性能差距，并诊断启发式方法中潜在的理论缺陷。

Method: (1) 利用BDAM中攻击者路径项的主导地位，通过下层级对偶化（lower-level dualization）实现精确的单层重构，得MILP可用于认证最优性；(2) 分析供给规则在平局情形下的病态性，提出具有平局不变性（tie-invariant）的威胁走廊（threat-corridor）公式；(3) 提出MILP-DA算法：将精确MILP锚点与走廊解码及可行性修复相结合。

Result: MILP在全部18个三行和五行配置上认证了最优性，其中17个在10秒内完成；在15×30网格上获得强非认证解。已发表的启发式平均值低于该前沿3.55%，且差距随规模扩大。平局移动对目标函数影响小于10^-3，但实现供给成本偏差高达0.83单位。MILP-DA在18个认证配置中赢得13个，在全部18个更大配置上全部获胜，败绩均出现在三行网格上。

Conclusion: BDAM具有可精确求解的单层等价形式，启发式解存在3.55%的平均最优性缺口并随规模扩大；供给规则在平局下病态但其影响被目标权重压制而非消除；将精确锚点与启发式解码结合的MILP-DA策略在多数算例上优于纯启发式，表明精确方法可作为有效增强启发式的手段。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [77] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中的策略迭代（PI）初始化问题，提出了一种新颖的谱赋值方法，通过Lyapunov型算子的谱逐步从稳定辅助系统逼近原始系统获得初始稳定控制器，并设计了无需初始稳定策略的模型自由算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代（PI）是解决最优控制问题的重要强化学习工具，但其初始化阶段（寻找初始稳定控制器）通常依赖于完整的模型信息。对于具有状态和控制相关乘性噪声的随机系统，稳定性由包含漂移项和扩散项的Lyapunov型不等式保证，而非确定性情形下的Hurwitz条件，这使得模型自由的PI初始化问题更具挑战性。

Method: 提出一种新的谱赋值方法：利用Lyapunov型算子的谱，通过调整累积因子使原始系统从稳定的辅助系统逐步逼近，从而获得稳定化控制增益。基于该方法，进一步利用系统数据并调整累积因子，设计了一种不依赖初始稳定策略的模型自由算法。

Result: 通过仿真结果验证了所提出的谱赋值方法和模型自由算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中模型自由PI的初始化难题，所提方法不需要初始稳定策略即可实现最优控制，为随机系统强化学习控制提供了新的解决途径。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式诊断方法未考虑传输延迟对故障诊断的影响，而实际系统中延迟是不可避免的，因此需要建立能够在有限步内即使在传输延迟条件下仍能诊断故障的验证方法。

Method: 提出一种扩展了文献中分布式可诊断性条件的新的分布式可诊断性条件，并利用名为"延迟记录器"（delay recorder）的新型结构和新的诊断函数来验证该条件。

Result: 理论分析表明所提出的验证方法能够成功确定故障是否可以被诊断。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [79] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文确定了弱受限增广Zarankiewicz数z_{wL}(m,3)在m=5,6,7,8,9时的精确值，全部满足z_{wL}(m,3)=2m，并通过这些结果导出BSR(m,3)≥2m的下界，支持了z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对所有m≥5成立的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱受限增广Zarankiewicz数是极值图论中的一个重要参数，与禁止C_4子图的图结构密切相关。作者旨在确定z_{wL}(m,3)在小参数范围内的精确值，以验证关于该参数线性增长（即等于2m）的猜想，并通过与BSR（二分歧义分辨率）数的联系为相关领域提供更紧的下界。

Method: 本文采用构造与精确搜索相结合的方法：(1) 通过显式的弱容许（weak admissible）构造给出下界，证明z_{wL}(m,3)≥2m；(2) 对于m=7,8,9，通过对规范的C_4-free基图进行精确有限搜索（exact finite searches）获得匹配的上界；(3) 结合已有的小情形结果，给出完整的证明链。

Result: 精确确定了z_{wL}(5,3)=10，z_{wL}(6,3)=12，z_{wL}(7,3)=14，z_{wL}(8,3)=16，z_{wL}(9,3)=18，即对所有5≤m≤9均有z_{wL}(m,3)=2m。此外，由BSR(m,n)≥z_{wL}(m,n)的不等式关系，得到BSR(m,3)≥2m对m=5,6,7,8,9成立。

Conclusion: 这些结果与小情形的已知结论共同强烈支持了猜想z_{wL}(m,3)=2m对所有m≥5成立。该工作不仅在理论上深化了对弱受限增广Zarankiewicz数的理解，也为BSR参数提供了新的下界，具有潜在的图论与信息论交叉应用价值。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [80] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机外梯度（SEG）方法在单调变分不等式问题（VIPs）上的收敛性，填补了同样本SEG（S-SEG）理论分析的空白，证明S-SEG对样本Lipschitz参数敏感、在非紧致域上的高概率收敛性，并揭示S-SEG与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）在非对称步长下收敛行为的本质差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外梯度算法是求解VIPs的基础算法，其确定性收敛理论已十分成熟，但随机版本的理论理解仍不充分。现有分析主要聚焦于独立样本SEG（I-SEG）且假设域紧致或随机算子方差一致有界，而同样本SEG（S-SEG）的收敛性质作为具有显著不同特性的自然变体几乎未被研究，这一理论空白亟需填补。

Method: 首先通过构造反例证明S-SEG对样本wise Lipschitz参数敏感，仅依靠均值Lipschitz性和有界方差无法保证其在紧致集上收敛；然后在可能无界的域上，在放宽的假设条件下为两种SEG变体建立高概率约束间隙（restricted-gap）收敛性分析；最后通过构造具体的随机单调VIP反例，证明对I-SEG保证几乎必然最后迭代收敛的非对称双步长选择对S-SEG会失效。

Result: (1) S-SEG在仅有均值Lipschitz性和有界方差假设下不收敛，即使域紧致；(2) 在放宽的假设下，两种SEG变体在无界域上均具有高概率的约束间隙收敛，且该结果在一般情况下不可本质改进；(3) 非对称双步长策略对S-SEG几乎必然发散，表明S-SEG与I-SEG在收敛行为上存在根本性差异。

Conclusion: S-SEG与I-SEG在收敛理论上具有本质区别：S-SEG对更强的样本级平滑性条件有内在需求，且其收敛性对步长策略更为敏感。本文的结果不仅给出了S-SEG的充分收敛条件，还通过反例揭示了现有方法对S-SEG的局限性，为后续设计专门的S-SEG收敛算法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>

*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从边际概率密度函数观测中推断广义常微分方程（ODE）动力学的框架。通过将低维观测到的扩散等不可逆随机过程重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，利用Liouville方程的特征线表示进行参数推断，并采用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器实现随机梯度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际系统中观测往往只能获得低维边际分布，而底层动力学可能包含不可观测的隐变量，且涉及随机或不可逆过程。直接推断这类系统的高维联合密度动力学非常困难，需要一种能够从边际观测中高效推断确定性ODE参数的方法。

Method: 将低维观测的扩散等不可逆过程重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，使联合密度满足双曲型Liouville方程；利用特征线表示避免直接求解高维Liouville方程；粒子沿特征线传播，通过沿特征线的敏感性传播计算欧拉敏感性；采用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计器，实现随机梯度下降推断参数。

Result: 通过四个实验验证方法有效性：（1）恢复三模态线性系统（仅观测单模态边际）；（2）含隐模态的非线性Gompertz增长模型；（3）隐模态将单峰边际变为双峰的双稳态系统；（4）细胞流中粒子的Stokes–Oseen阻力定律恢复。并在这些设置中分析了收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将不可逆或随机动力学的推断任务转化为从边际观测中恢复确定性ODE的问题，特征线表示方法避免了高维联合密度的直接求解，交叉U统计量保证了梯度估计的无偏性，从而实现了高效且可收敛的参数推断。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [82] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化方法在Stiefel流形（正交列矩阵流形）上的应用。作者证明了Stiefel Muon更新存在精确的闭式解，并基于此提出了Skewon算法，用于正交约束优化，同时建立了平滑非凸设定下的一阶收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon是一种矩阵感知的优化方法，其在Stiefel流形上的现有扩展依赖于启发式、近似或迭代更新，计算效率各不相同。缺乏精确且高效的闭式解，这限制了Muon方法在正交约束优化问题中的实际应用。

Method: 作者首先推导了Stiefel流形上Muon更新步骤的精确闭式解，然后基于该闭式解设计了Skewon算法，实现了正交约束优化的高效计算。

Result: Skewon算法在Stiefel流形上具备高效实现，并在平滑非凸优化设定下建立了一阶收敛性保证。

Conclusion: 本文为Muon在Stiefel流形上的应用提供了精确且高效的解决方案（Skewon），弥补了现有方法启发式或近似更新的不足，并给出了收敛性理论保障。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [83] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界域上多维连续性方程（带源项）通过局部内部通量反馈的镇定问题。在有限时间特征阻尼不等式等几何条件下，结合Lyapunov型入口条件和增益条件，证明了误差在L2范数下的指数稳定性，并给出了加权Lyapunov泛函下的输入到状态（ISS）估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多维连续性方程（含源项）在有界域上的稳定性控制问题，需要设计有效的局部通量反馈策略，使得误差动态相较于参考剖面满足带局部阻尼的输运方程，从而实现镇定。

Method: (1) 通过散度形式进行局部内部通量反馈设计；(2) 提出有限时间特征阻尼不等式作为主要几何条件，要求特征线在时间窗口内累积足够的阻尼；(3) 引入Lyapunov型入口条件来保证支撑受限轨迹族上的特征阻尼；(4) 构造加权Lyapunov泛函，建立微分Lyapunov准则，推导ISS估计；(5) 讨论椭圆右逆实现反馈通量，并将特征阻尼论证推广到非线性依赖于状态的速度场。

Result: 在增益条件（局部阻尼与输运场的压缩放大相关联）下，证明了误差的L2指数稳定性；建立了输入到状态（ISS）估计以应对加性扰动；并给出了二维示例验证几何、增益及实现条件。

Conclusion: 本文为多维连续性方程的局部通量反馈镇定提供了完整的理论框架，涵盖稳定性证明、扰动鲁棒性和非线性能推广，并通过二维实例验证了所提条件的有效性。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实系统中，观测信息往往存在传输延迟，而现有的故障诊断与预测理论大多假设观测即时到达。这种假设不切实际，且若直接套用经典的可诊断性和可预测性概念会导致保守的结果，因此需要针对延迟观测场景重新定义和验证相关条件。

Method: 提出了两种在延迟观测条件下诊断和预测的充分必要条件；设计了延迟观测器来建模观测延迟的影响；引入一个全新的验证函数来检验可诊断性和可预测性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明所提出的验证方法能够有效验证延迟观测下离散事件系统的故障诊断与预测条件，并且修正后的可预测性概念克服了经典定义的保守性。

Conclusion: 在延迟观测场景下，通过延迟观测器和新的验证函数可以系统地验证系统的可诊断性和可预测性，修订后的可预测性概念更贴合实际并避免了保守性。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了将稳定措施（惯量与电压稳定性约束）与发输电及储能扩展规划同步进行的集成式电力系统规划方法，发现相较于传统序贯规划方法，集成方法可降低系统成本，且电网成形电池储能系统的双重用途优于STATCOM等稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器资源渗透率不断提高，同步发电机对动态电压稳定和惯性响应的贡献需要被替代。然而现有的电力系统规划方法是序贯式的，稳定措施仅在规划后期才被考虑，无法充分评估和优化稳定措施的价值。

Method: 提出一种集成式电力系统规划方法，在发电、输电和储能系统扩展规划的同时，通过简化的惯量与电压稳定性约束，同步考虑稳定措施（如电网成形电池储能系统和STATCOM）的部署。该方法将稳定约束嵌入到规划优化模型中，实现稳定措施与系统扩张的协同优化。

Result: 研究发现集成式规划方法能够降低系统总成本；同时，电网成形电池储能系统因其双重用途（既提供稳定支撑又可用于能量调度）而优于STATCOM等仅用于稳定的单一功能设备。

Conclusion: 集成式电力系统规划相比传统序贯规划更具经济优势，应优先考虑将稳定性约束前置到规划阶段，并推广电网成形电池储能系统的双重用途以提升系统成本效率。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>

### [86] [Agentic Nesting: A New Methodology for Existing Enterprise Application Integration and Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05159)
*Xi Wang,Kun Li,Xianyao Ling,Gang Yin,Liang Zhang,Jiang Wu,Wenbo Lei,Jun Xu,Annie Wang,Fu Zhang,Weizhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Nesting，一个多智能体协作框架，将现有企业应用封装为自治AI智能体并组织成分层嵌套的监管拓扑结构，通过中心编排器协调多智能体进行任务分解与动态调度，并暴露统一对话式接口，实现跨应用查询与流程编排，提出"应用即智能体"集成范式和"对话即集成"交互哲学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业运营依赖多个异构业务系统和信息应用，导致严重的数据孤岛与流程碎片化。传统企业应用集成方法（如ESB中间件、API网关、RPA）存在架构耦合度高、运维成本攀升和智能化能力有限等固有缺陷，亟需新的集成范式来有效利用和编排这些投入巨大的企业应用。

Method: 该框架从每个遗留应用中提取数字智能体代理，实现自然语言交互和自主操作能力；通过中心编排器协调多个智能体进行任务分解与动态调度；将智能体组织成分层监管拓扑结构（而非扁平互联）；对外暴露统一对话式接口以支持跨应用查询和流程编排。

Result: 本文的主要贡献在于提出了"应用即智能体"（Application-as-Agent）的集成范式和"对话即集成"（Conversation-as-Integration）的交互哲学，并探索了该方法论在异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: Agentic Nesting框架通过层级化多智能体协作结构替代传统扁平集成方式，为企业应用集成提供了一种更解耦、更智能化、运维成本更低的解决方案，并在多种异构系统协调和大规模数据应用场景中展现出良好的推广前景。

Abstract: Enterprise operations extensively rely on multiple heterogeneous business systems and information applications, which also result in severe data silos and process fragmentation. Enterprises have invested considerable financial and material resources in building these applications, however, effectively leveraging and orchestrating them remains a formidable challenge. Conventional approaches to enterprise application integration, encompassing middleware architectures such as Enterprise Service Bus (ESB), API gateway infrastructures, and Robotic Process Automation (RPA), suffer from inherent limitations like high architectural coupling, escalating operation and maintenance costs, and limited intelligence capabilities. This paper proposes Agentic Nesting, a multi-agent collaboration framework in which existing enterprise applications are encapsulated as autonomous AI agents within a hierarchically nested structure. Rather than flat interconnection, agents are organized into layered stewardship topologies that mirror the compositional complexity of enterprise ecosystems. The framework extracts a digital agent proxy from each legacy application to enable natural-language interaction and autonomous manipulation, coordinates multiple agents through a central orchestrator for task decomposition and dynamic dispatching, and exposes a unified conversational interface for cross-application querying and process orchestration. The main contributions of this paper are the proposition of the "Application-as-Agent" integration paradigm and the "Conversation-as-Integration" interaction philosophy, together with an exploration of the generalization potential of this methodology in scenarios encompassing heterogeneous system coordination, and large-scale data applications.

</details>


### [87] [The Ignition Index: Measuring Global Workspace Dynamics in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05160)
*Saman Rahbar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出点火指数（Ignition Index），一个基于全局工作空间理论（GWT）的标量指标，用于量化Transformer语言模型中信息处理的全有或全无（all-or-none）点火转变。该指标通过对每层线性探针准确率与输入信号强度的关系拟合四参数sigmoid提取陡峭度参数β-hat，在11个模型、5个架构家族上验证了9.6倍的选择性（p<0.001）。主要发现包括：前馈Transformer在聚合β-hat上比SSM高89%；Mamba呈近线性轮廓；Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴点火强度为深度轴的2.12倍；Pythia-410M在第256个训练步骤出现相位转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桥接全局工作空间理论（GWT）的动力学预测与机械可解释性研究之间长期存在的鸿沟。GWT预测认知信息处理存在全有或全无的点火现象，但此前缺乏在Transformer语言模型中验证该理论的量化指标，也缺乏跨架构间系统性的比较工具。

Method: 提出Ignition Index (I)指标：对每层线性探针准确率随输入信号强度变化的曲线拟合四参数sigmoid函数，提取陡峭度参数β-hat。高β-hat表示突变的点火式转变，低β-hat表示渐进的积累。使用shuffled-label对照验证指标对真实语言结构的选择性，并在11个模型（涵盖5种架构家族）上进行系统评估，采用Mann-Whitney U检验和Cohen's d进行统计比较，使用PELT算法检测训练过程中的相位转变。

Result: 四个核心发现：(1) 前馈Transformer在聚合β-hat上比SSM高89%（p<1e-13, d=0.52），Mamba显示近线性分布，符合缺乏全局广播的特征；(2) Huginn-3.5B沿迭代轴的点火强度是深度轴的2.12倍，说明循环架构沿循环维度展现工作空间式转变；(3) Pythia-410M在第256个训练步骤检测到相位转变（+67%），先于归纳头的形成；(4) 点火与模型规模和信号强度的假设未得到证实，表明Transformer架构可能已饱和其可用的点火机制。整体指标具有9.6倍测量选择性。

Conclusion: Ignition Index首次提供了经过验证的量化桥梁，连接GWT的动力学预测与机械可解释性。该指标具有9.6倍测量选择性和架构级别的判别能力，这在以往扩展性文献中未被刻画。研究揭示了不同架构家族在信息处理模式上的根本差异，并为理解循环模型中沿时间维度的认知涌现提供了新视角。

Abstract: We introduce the Ignition Index (I), a validated scalar metric that operationalizes Global Workspace Theory's (GWT) all-or-none ignition prediction in transformer language models. The metric fits a four-parameter sigmoid to per-layer linear probe accuracy as a function of input signal strength, extracting steepness parameter beta-hat: high values indicate abrupt, ignition-like transitions; low values indicate graded build-up. Across 11 models spanning five architecture families, shuffled-label controls demonstrate 9.6-fold selectivity for genuine linguistic structure over spurious probe capacity (p < 0.001, Mann-Whitney U-test). We find: (1) Feedforward transformers exceed SSMs by 89% in aggregate beta-hat (p < 1e-13, Cohen's d = 0.52), with Mamba exhibiting near-linear profiles consistent with absent global broadcast. (2) Huginn-3.5B exhibits 2.12-fold higher ignition along its iteration axis than its depth axis, demonstrating that recurrent architectures manifest workspace-like transitions along the recurrence dimension. (3) Pythia-410M shows a PELT-detected phase transition at training step 256 (+67%), preceding induction-head formation. (4) Hypotheses linking ignition to model scale and signal strength were not confirmed, suggesting transformer architectures may saturate available ignition mechanisms. The Ignition Index provides the first validated quantitative bridge between GWT's dynamical predictions and mechanistic interpretability, with 9.6-fold measurement selectivity and architecture-level discriminability not previously characterized in the scaling literature. Code: https://github.com/saman-rahbar/ignition-index

</details>


### [88] [Woodpecker Distillation: Weak Models Diagnose Reasoning Bugs in Strong Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05168)
*Dayu Wang,Jiaye Yang,Weikang Li,Jiahui Liang,Yang Li,Deguo Xia,Jizhou Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Woodpecker Distillation框架，利用弱模型生成局部对比性补丁来修复强模型在推理任务中的局部错误，并通过蒸馏纠正性教师分布来提升强模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理任务中经常失败，但这些失败往往源于中间步骤的局部推理错误而非整体能力不足。这些局部错误可以通过插入短补丁来修复，但直接的微调方法无法有效内化这种修复信号，因为有用的信号在于修复如何重塑模型的未来推理分布，而非干预文本本身。

Method: 提出Woodpecker Distillation，一种弱到强的训练框架。该方法在相同前缀处对比成功和不成功的弱模型补丁，构建一个纠正性的教师分布（基于补丁诱导的未来token预测），然后将该信号蒸馏到强模型中。

Result: 在数学推理基准上的实验表明，Woodpecker Distillation持续提升了强模型性能，且优于直接模仿基线方法。

Conclusion: 局部修复信号的有效利用应关注其如何重塑模型的推理分布而非干预文本本身，Woodpecker Distillation通过对比性局部干预的蒸馏方式，成功将弱模型的纠正信号传递给强模型并提升推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models often fail on reasoning tasks despite possessing the capability to solve them. We argue that many such failures arise from localized reasoning bugs in intermediate steps rather than from global incompetence. We show that these bugs are frequently repairable: inserting a short patch generated by a weak probe model after the same strong-model reasoning prefix can redirect the trajectory toward a correct solution.
  However, this corrective effect is not reliably internalized by directly fine-tuning on weak patches or repaired trajectories, suggesting that the useful signal lies not in the intervention text itself, but in how it reshapes the model's future reasoning distribution. We therefore propose Woodpecker Distillation, a weak-to-strong training framework that learns from contrastive local interventions. Our method contrasts successful and unsuccessful weak-model patches at the same prefix, constructs a corrective teacher distribution from their induced future token predictions, and distills this signal into the strong model.
  Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that Woodpecker Distillation consistently improves strong-model performance and outperforms direct imitation baselines.

</details>


### [89] [From Continuous Predictors to Clinical Thresholds: Early Evidence on Performance Trade-offs of Guideline-Based Categorisation for Ischaemic Stroke Outcome Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05203)
*Esra Zihni,Katryna Cisek,Hamzah Ziadeh,Hendrik Knoche,Robert Mikulik,John D. Kelleher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测中，将连续型预测因子替换为基于临床指南的分类型编码是否会影响模型性能。在多中心欧洲注册数据上，完全分类化的梯度提升模型在两个治疗队列中与连续特征模型统计上无差异，仅在一个队列中出现显著性能下降，且全局特征重要性排序保持一致。基于指南的分类化是卒中结局模型可行的设计选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在急性缺血性卒中90天结局预测上已有较强预测精度，但临床应用受限，原因是模型解释与临床医生的推理方式不一致。一项临床医生用户研究呼吁采用与临床指南对齐的截断值，这促使作者探索是否可以在不损失性能的前提下，将连续预测因子替换为临床知情（指南对齐）的分类编码。

Method: 基于多中心欧洲卒中注册数据，将患者按三种治疗方式分为三个队列。对比标准梯度提升模型（使用连续特征）与完全分类化的梯度提升模型（使用基于卒中指南的、治疗特异性的截断阈值进行分类），评估两者的预测性能差异及全局特征重要性排序的一致性。

Result: 在三个治疗队列中，完全分类化模型在两个队列中与连续特征模型在统计上不可区分，在一个队列中出现显著的预测精度下降。全局特征重要性排序在所有治疗组中保持一致，说明将连续预测因子离散化为指南分类不会破坏预后因素的核心层级结构。

Conclusion: 基于指南的分类化方法对卒中结局预测模型而言是可行的设计选择：在保持模型可解释性（与临床推理对齐）的同时，大多数场景下不牺牲预测性能，且保持了全局特征重要性的层级结构。

Abstract: Machine learning models achieve strong predictive accuracy for 90-day outcome prediction in acute ischaemic stroke, yet clinical adoption is limited by the misalignment of model explanations with clinicians' reasoning. Motivated by a clinician user study calling for clinical guideline-aligned cut-offs, we ask whether continuous predictors can be replaced by clinically informed categorical encodings without sacrificing performance. On a multi-centre European registry stratified into three treatment cohorts, we compare standard and fully categorised gradient-boosted models, the latter using stroke guideline-aligned, treatment-specific thresholds. The fully categorised models are statistically indistinguishable from their continuous counterparts in two of the treatment cohorts, with a significant drop in predictive accuracy in one cohort. Global feature importance rankings remain consistent, suggesting that discretising continuous predictors into guideline-based categories preserves the core hierarchy of prognostic factors across all treatment groups. Guideline-based categorisation is thus a viable design choice for stroke-outcome models.

</details>


### [90] [SkillTrace: Multi-Trace Provenance Auditing for LLM-Agent Skill Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05204)
*Jialuo Chen,Minghe Wang,Lingqi Jiang,Jianan Ma,Xinhao Deng,Xiaohu Du,Ruixiao Lin,Yunhao Feng,Linkang Du,Jingyi Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SKILLTRACE是一个面向LLM-agent技能复用的多轨迹溯源审计框架，通过提取表达式、实现和操作三条轨迹（其中操作轨迹用Skill Operational Graph表示），在审计时确定性比对缓存的轨迹数据，实现精确的技能复用检测。在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准上达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent生态系统中可复用技能正在快速增长，技能成为市场化的工件，但现有检测器仅针对单模态源代码或整体包相似度，无法捕获仅保留技能部分特征的复用证据，因为技能复用证据分散在文本、实现片段和操作结构中。

Method: SKILLTRACE提取三条溯源轨迹：表达式轨迹（Expression）、实现轨迹（Implementation）和操作轨迹（Operational）。其中操作轨迹通过Skill Operational Graph（SOG）表示，捕获激活、流程和资源流结构。LLM仅在摄取时辅助一次操作轨迹提取，审计时系统对缓存轨迹进行确定性对比，并针对同功能严格负样本校准每条轨迹，最终报告哪条轨迹支持复用判定。

Result: 在SKILLTRACE-BENCH基准（包含820个变换复用正样本、100个市场锚点和751个负对照）上达到AUROC 0.938和F1 0.898。在包含36,446个技能的野外观测审计中，基于轨迹归因的证据生成了超出仓库级基线的可操作复用审查队列。

Conclusion: SKILLTRACE通过多轨迹溯源审计框架有效解决了LLM-agent技能复用检测中多模态证据分散的问题，实验证明其准确性和实用性优于仓库级基线，为技能市场化的可审计性提供了新方案。

Abstract: LLM-agent ecosystems are rapidly growing around reusable skills: mixed-modality packages of metadata, natural-language instructions, code, tools, references, and operational workflows. As skills become marketplace artifacts, auditing their reuse is no longer the same problem as ordinary code clone detection. Existing detectors target single-modality source code or whole-package similarity, yet skill reuse evidence is distributed across authored text, implementation fragments, and operational structure. As a result, they can miss reuse that preserves only one part of a skill. We present SKILLTRACE, a multi-trace provenance auditing framework for LLM-agent skill reuse. SKILLTRACE extracts three provenance traces: Expression, Implementation, and Operational. It represents the Operational Trace as a Skill Operational Graph (SOG) that captures activation, procedure, and resource-flow structure. An LLM assists only the Operational-trace extraction, once at ingestion; at audit time SKILLTRACE compares cached traces deterministically, calibrates each trace against same-function strict negatives, and reports which trace supports a reuse decision. On SKILLTRACE-BENCH, with 820 transformed reuse positives over 100 marketplace anchors and 751 negative controls, SKILLTRACE achieves AUROC 0.938 and F1 0.898. A 36,446-skill wild audit further shows that trace-attributed evidence surfaces actionable reuse review queues beyond repository-level baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Abstract Event Causal Rules: Induction and Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05205)
*Ziwei Zheng,Peiqiong Chen,Bang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR）范式，将具体因果事件对转化为广义抽象因果逻辑，并设计多智能体CACI系统提取可信AECR知识库，最终通过AR-GCAE模型注入因果图事件预测任务，显著提升低频长尾和未见事件组合的泛化与预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向事件中心的智能分析系统依赖显式因果事件知识，但现有实例级因果对在低频长尾和未见事件组合上存在严重的泛化缺陷，亟需一种更抽象、更稳健的因果表示范式来克服这一局限。

Method: 提出抽象事件因果规则（AECR）关系级因果抽象范式；设计多智能体具体到抽象因果归纳（CACI）系统，结合相似度约束聚类从噪声原始因果数据中蒸馏可信AECR，构建两个完整AECR知识库；进一步提出抽象规则引导的因果注意力编码器（AR-GCAE），通过规则引导注意力层与门控表示融合将检索到的AECR注入因果图事件预测（CGEP）任务。

Result: 定量实验表明，应用AECR显著增强了事件因果推理的泛化能力，为事件预测带来一致性的性能提升，其中在稀有和未见事件样本上取得了最显著的增益。

Conclusion: 抽象因果规则能够弥补实例级因果对的泛化不足，有效提升事件因果推理与预测在长尾及未见场景下的表现，验证了抽象因果知识的实用价值。

Abstract: Event-centric intelligent analytical systems heavily depend on explicit causal event knowledge for risk early warning, decision-making support and narrative comprehension. Nevertheless, existing instance-level causal pairs suffer severe generalization deficits on low-frequency long-tail and unseen event combinations. To address this limitation, this work proposes Abstract Event Causal Rule (AECR), a novel relation-level causal abstraction paradigm that transforms concrete cause-effect pairs into generalized abstract causal logic while retaining their intrinsic causal relationships. We design a multi-agent Concrete-to-Abstract Causal Induction (CACI) system coupled with similarity-constrained clustering to distill trustworthy AECRs from noisy raw causal data, based on which two complete AECR knowledge bases are built. To validate the practical utility of abstract causal knowledge, we propose an Abstract Rule-Guided Causal Attention Encoder (AR-GCAE), which injects the retrieved AECRs into the causality Graph Event Prediction (CGEP) benchmark task via rule-guided attention layers and gated representation fusion. Quantitative experimental results reveal that applying AECRs substantially strengthens the generalization capacity of event causal reasoning and brings consistent performance improvements to event prediction, with the most prominent gains observed on rare and unseen event samples.

</details>


### [92] [Otter: A Time-Aware, History-Conditioned Human Chess AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05206)
*Tarun Kumar S*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Otter是一个1530万参数的人类象棋AI，通过将棋局建模为时间感知的顺序过程而非孤立地处理每个局面来预测人类走子。它结合了最近20步的历史编码器和时钟压力调节模块，在61亿个局面（来自1.17亿局Lichess快棋）上训练，仅用单张T4 GPU训练30天，达到55.23%的top-1和90.95%的top-5走子预测准确率，超越了此前最先进的人类象棋模型Maia 2，且参数更少、训练数据更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人类象棋AI模型（如Maia 2）将每个棋局位置独立处理，忽略了棋局的时间维度——包括走子历史的顺序信息和时钟压力对人的决策影响。作者认为，将国际象棋建模为时间感知的顺序活动，能够更准确地捕捉人类走子选择背后的行为模式，从而超越仅基于位置的方法。

Method: Otter采用两种条件信号：(1) 走子历史编码器——基于最近20步棋进行预测，捕获开局偏好、局面漂移和局内行为倾向；(2) 时间控制模块——根据时钟压力动态调节预测输出。模型在61亿个局面上训练，数据来自1.17亿局Lichess快棋对局。

Result: Otter在top-1和top-5走子预测上分别达到55.23%和90.95%的准确率，超越此前最先进的人类象棋模型Maia 2，且参数更少、训练数据更少。在11个Elo区间（<1100到>=2000）中，准确率在1900-1999区间达到峰值57.38%。

Conclusion: 将国际象棋建模为时间感知、顺序性的活动，使用更小的模型即可获得比仅基于位置的方法更高的人类走子预测准确率。这一结果表明，棋类AI在模拟人类决策时应纳入时间动态信息。

Abstract: Otter is a 15.3M-parameter human chess AI that predicts human move selection by modeling play as a time-aware, sequential process rather than treating each position in isolation. It combines two conditioning signals: (1) a move history encoder that conditions predictions on the last 20 moves, capturing opening preferences, positional drift, and intra-game behavioral tendencies; and (2) a time control module that modulates predictions based on clock pressure. Otter is trained on 6.1 billion positions from 117 million Lichess rapid games over 30 days on a single T4 GPU.
  Otter achieves 55.23% top-1 and 90.95% top-5 move-prediction accuracy, surpassing the prior state-of-the-art human chess model, Maia 2, with far fewer parameters and less training data. Across 11 Elo brackets (<1100 to >=2000), accuracy peaks at 57.38% in the 1900-1999 bracket. These results show that modeling chess as a time-aware, sequential activity yields more human-accurate move prediction than position-only approaches, using a smaller model. Code, trained models, and complete training logs are publicly released.

</details>


### [93] [SearchAuditor: Auditing and Attributing Failures in Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05212)
*Zhixiang Liang,Yifei Liu,Yidan Huang,Haozhe Zhao,Beichen Huang,Jiaqi Wang,Nan Duan,Qiong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SearchAuditBench基准测试，用于评估LLM审计员定位、归因并修复深度搜索代理失败的能力，同时提出SearchAuditor多视角审计框架。实验表明即使在GPT-5.5等前沿模型驱动下，最强基线端到端通过率仅26.6%，而SearchAuditor达到32.3%并能使代理从错误中更好恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度搜索代理通过长时间跨度的网页交互解决复杂问题，过程复杂且脆弱：小的推理错误可能通过长而有噪声的轨迹传播为流畅但错误的答案。诊断这类失败需要手动检查极长的执行轨迹，超出人类能力范围，因此需要自动化审计工具来减轻人类负担。

Method: 构建SearchAuditBench基准，包含1,243条失败轨迹（平均73.1条消息、65.1K个token），来自8个开源模型在5个深度搜索基准上的表现，每条轨迹由专家标注关键错误步骤、搜索特定根因及带评分规则的参考修复方案。同时提出SearchAuditor，一种基于证据裁决的多视角审计框架，有效实现失败定位、归因和修复。

Result: 即使在GPT-5.5前沿模型驱动下，最强基线仅达到26.6%的端到端通过率；SearchAuditor在不同前沿模型上持续优于所有基线，达到32.3%的端到端通过率，且使用其修复方案恢复失败运行能使代理更好地从错误中恢复。

Conclusion: 搜索代理失败审计仍然极具挑战性，但多视角、证据驱动的审计框架SearchAuditor显著提升了定位、归因和修复的能力，验证了自动化审计在减少人类负担方面的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Deep search agents tackle challenging questions through long-horizon web interactions, a process that is both complex and fragile: small reasoning errors may propagate through long, noisy trajectories into fluent but incorrect answers. Diagnosing such failures is difficult, requiring the manual inspection of extremely long execution traces, which could be beyond human capacity. We therefore introduce SearchAuditBench, a benchmark that evaluates whether LLM auditors can localize, attribute, and repair these failures, thereby reducing the human burden. SearchAuditBench comprises 1,243 failed trajectories, averaging 73.1 messages and 65.1K tokens, collected from eight open-weight models on five deep-search benchmarks, each expert-annotated with the critical error step, a search-specific root cause, and a reference repair with grading rubrics. We further propose SearchAuditor, a multi-perspective auditing framework that effectively localizes, attributes, and repairs search-agent failures through evidence-grounded adjudication. Experimental results show that even the strongest baseline, when powered by a frontier model like GPT-5.5, attains only a 26.6% end-to-end pass rate. In contrast, our SearchAuditor consistently outperforms all baselines across different frontier models, achieving an end-to-end pass rate of 32.3%, and resuming failed runs with its repairs enables agents to better recover from errors.

</details>


### [94] [PD-GS: Phoneme-Driven 3DGS for Audio-Driven Talking Heads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05218)
*Ao Fu,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PD-GS提出了一种基于音素驱动的高斯泼溅（3DGS）说话人头像生成方法，通过时间对齐的音素令牌与连续音频上下文的自适应融合，解决了唇部运动过平滑和"漏嘴"伪影问题，在HDTF数据集上取得了最优的唇部几何指标（LMD 2.66）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS说话人渲染虽能实现快速逼真的效果，但唇部发音精度不足：回归目标下连续声学嵌入偏向平均值，导致唇动过度平滑，无法精确捕捉双唇闭塞等短暂离散的发音事件，产生"漏嘴"伪影；而自监督语音编码器虽提供丰富的韵律和音系线索，但缺乏帧对齐的明确语言目标来可靠地消解闭唇级事件。

Method: PD-GS包含两大核心：1）利用ASR和强制对齐流水线获取时间对齐的音素令牌，为3DGS说话人提供显式语言目标；2）设计语言融合模块（LFM），通过可学习门控机制自适应融合连续音频上下文与离散音素嵌入，在发音关键片段强化音素引导，同时保留音频驱动的平滑动态。模型仅依赖单目视频，通过图像重建和唇部关键点监督进行训练。

Result: 在HDTF数据集上，PD-GS在唇部几何方面超越所有对比基线，LMD指标达到2.66；在具有挑战性的音素序列中定性减少了闭唇违规事件，生成更具语言学保真度的神经头像。

Conclusion: 通过引入时间对齐的音素令牌与自适应的音素-音频融合机制，PD-GS有效缓解了说话人头像生成中的唇部过平滑问题，验证了离散语言先验对于提升发音精度的重要性，为更忠实于语言学的神经虚拟人生成提供了新的思路。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables fast, photorealistic talking-head rendering, yet accurate lip articulation remains elusive: mouth motion is often over-smoothed and may violate hard articulatory constraints such as bilabial closures, producing the notorious ``leaky mouth'' artifact. A key difficulty is that brief, discrete articulatory events are inferred from a continuous acoustic embedding under a regression objective, which biases predictions toward averaged mouth configurations. While modern self-supervised speech encoders provide rich prosodic and phonetic cues, they do not provide an explicit, frame-aligned linguistic target that reliably disambiguates closure-level events. We propose \textbf{Phoneme-Driven Gaussian Splatting (PD-GS)}, which augments a 3DGS talker with time-aligned phoneme tokens obtained from an automatic ASR and forced-alignment pipeline. Our core component, the \textbf{Linguistic Fusion Module (LFM)}, adaptively fuses continuous audio context with discrete phoneme embeddings through a learned gate, allowing the model to preserve smooth audio-driven dynamics while strengthening phoneme guidance on articulation-critical segments. PD-GS is trained purely from monocular video using image reconstruction and lip landmark supervision. On HDTF, PD-GS achieves the best lip geometry among the compared baselines (LMD 2.66) and qualitatively reduces closure violations in challenging phoneme sequences, yielding more linguistically faithful neural avatars.

</details>


### [95] [When Privileged Guidance Misaligns: State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation for Multi-Turn Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05219)
*Junzhuo Liu,Weiwei Li,Jun Ling,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SMRC-SD方法，通过状态匹配路由和有上下文的自蒸馏，解决特权蒸馏中教师参考轨迹与学生实际执行状态不匹配的问题，在ALFWorld和WebShop上显著提升多轮智能体任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 特权在线策略蒸馏通过同步教师在每个回合重新评分学生响应来提供密集监督，但在交互式环境中，学生先前的动作会持续改变执行状态，导致其rollout可能到达参考轨迹未覆盖的状态，使参考成为不可靠的指导来源，造成状态-参考不匹配。这一不匹配促使核心目标：提供与学生当前执行状态兼容的特权参考指导。

Method: SMRC-SD方法包含两个关键机制：一是状态匹配路由（State-Matched Routing），在每个回合验证学生当前执行状态是否与参考轨迹中的某个受支持状态匹配，仅在匹配状态处应用蒸馏，过滤掉参考缺乏局部兼容指导的回合；二是上下文化的自蒸馏（Contextualized Self-Distillation），对于每个匹配状态，从成功轨迹构建状态条件化的教师上下文，将监督锚定在实际到达的状态上。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上，SMRC-SD一致优于无条件的成功全路径蒸馏。使用Qwen3-1.7B，ALFWorld任务成功率从0.746提升至0.865，WebShop从0.574提升至0.693。受控的路由和上下文消融实验证实，选择局部支持的回合和构建状态兼容的教师上下文都是贡献增益的因素。

Conclusion: SMRC-SD通过显式判断特权轨迹何时以及如何指导在线策略学生，有效解决了状态下匹配问题，在多个环境上取得显著性能提升，验证了状态匹配路由和状态条件化教师上下文两个组件的有效性。

Abstract: Privileged on-policy distillation provides dense supervision for multi-turn agents by allowing a synchronized teacher to re-score the student's response at every turn with access to training-only references, such as successful trajectories. In interactive environments, however, the student's preceding actions continually change the execution state. As the student takes different actions or completes subgoals in a different order, its rollout may reach states not covered by the reference, making the reference an unreliable source of guidance for the state actually reached. Applying privileged distillation indiscriminately therefore creates state--reference mismatch. This mismatch motivates a central objective: providing privileged reference guidance that remains compatible with the student's current execution state. We introduce State-Matched Routing and Contextualized Self-Distillation (SMRC-SD), which explicitly determines when and how a privileged trajectory should guide an on-policy student. At each turn, SMRC-SD verifies whether the student's current execution state matches a supported state along the reference trajectory. Distillation is applied only at matched states, filtering out turns for which the reference lacks locally compatible guidance. For each matched state, SMRC-SD further constructs state-conditioned teacher context from the successful trajectory, grounding supervision in the state actually reached. Across ALFWorld and WebShop, SMRC-SD consistently outperforms unconditional successful full-path distillation. With Qwen3-1.7B, it improves task success from $0.746$ to $0.865$ on ALFWorld and from $0.574$ to $0.693$ on WebShop. Controlled routing and context ablations support both selecting locally supported turns and constructing state-compatible teacher context as contributors to these gains. Code is available at https://github.com/liujunzhuo/SMRC-SD.

</details>


### [96] [Small Foundation Models of Human Cognition and Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05224)
*Nick Oh,Fernand Gobet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文训练了14个从135M到14B参数的大语言模型，使用4种架构家族和包含1070万次试验的人类行为数据集Psych-101。结果显示：在分布内，模型规模几乎不影响性能，0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线；但在分布外，更大模型在泛化到新任务结构时具有显著优势。通过逐步剥离提示通道和置乱试验顺序的诊断实验，发现刺激内容和反馈信息对于性能至关重要，而选择历史本身不足以解释模型表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型作为认知代理的研究中，有两个悬而未决的问题：一是需要多大模型规模才能有效模拟人类行为；二是这些模型究竟是真正处理了任务结构，还是仅仅利用了统计捷径。作者希望通过大规模系统实验回答这两个关键问题。

Method: 作者构建了Psych-101数据集（来自160个心理实验的1070万次试次级选择数据），训练了14个模型（135M到14B参数，覆盖4种架构家族）。评估方式包括：(1) 分布内评估（匹配训练中见过的参与者）和分布外评估（泛化到新任务结构）；(2) 通过逐步剥离四个提示通道（任务指令、实验刺激、结果反馈、选择历史）跨27个实验进行归因分析；(3) 对试验顺序进行置换以测试对序列依赖的敏感性。

Result: 分布内模型性能饱和在狭窄区间，0.6B-1B参数即可匹配70B基线；分布外呈现更陡峭的缩放梯度，大模型泛化优势明显。遮蔽刺激和反馈内容后，75.7%的学习信息被破坏且模型性能跌至随机水平以下。置换试验顺序的结果表明：独立试验任务对顺序不敏感，而依赖先前反应的顺序性任务则高度敏感。

Conclusion: 小型认知微调语言模型总体内性能已接近饱和，可作为心理实验的噪声天花板估计器；但其泛化能力和适用范围受限于训练中见过的范式。模型的性能主要依赖刺激内容和反馈信息，而非单纯的选择历史统计捷径。

Abstract: Large language models fine-tuned on human behavioural data have emerged as general-purpose cognitive proxies, but the scale this requires, and whether these models process task structure or exploit statistical shortcuts, remain open questions. We train fourteen models from 135M to 14B parameters across four architecture families on Psych-101, a dataset of 10.7 million trial-level choices from 160 experiments. In-distribution, scale barely matters. The models fall within a narrow band, as though against a ceiling, and 0.6B to 1B parameters suffice to match a 70B baseline on held-out participants. Out-of-distribution, that band opens into a markedly steeper scaling gradient, with larger models clearly advantaged in generalisation to novel task structure. To determine what information these models use, we run two diagnostics. We progressively strip four prompt channels -- task instructions, experimental stimuli, outcome feedback, and choice history -- across 27 experiments, and permute trial order. Masking the content of stimuli and feedback destroys 75.7% of learned information and pushes models below chance, demonstrating that choice history alone does not account for performance. Permutation reveals invariance on tasks with independent trials but sensitivity where trial order is determined by prior responses. Small cognitively fine-tuned models therefore show promise as noise ceiling estimators for psychological experiments, though their scope remains bounded by the paradigms seen in training.

</details>


### [97] [Project2Task: Graph-Guided Project-Level Planning for Autonomous Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05225)
*Huirui Xu,Runtao Xu,Shuo Ren,Jiajun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Project2Task，一个图引导的项目级规划层，将研究项目自动分解为有依赖关系的、边界清晰的子任务契约，显著提升任务组合的质量和下流执行准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究代理系统将研究项目视为单一超大任务，产生模糊或重叠的任务列表，缺乏对任务边界和依赖顺序的明确规划，项目级工作难以获得连贯、可执行的任务分解。

Method: Project2Task将候选贡献表示为创新原子（innovation atoms）并组织成有向谱系图；通过轻量级Bernoulli块模型目标选择水平、垂直或混合组合分解；随后生成有明确贡献归属的有界任务，修复重叠和缺失执行字段，输出含目标、输入、预期产物、评估要求、边界约束、依赖和执行顺序的依赖感知任务契约。这些契约独立于任何下流研究执行器。

Result: 在10个项目简报约30个任务的基准上，Project2Task平均质量得分7.15（Brief Baseline为4.58，Topic-only Setting为5.31）；与AutoResearchClaw集成使下流任务平均准确率从0.536提升至0.759。

Conclusion: 显式的项目到任务规划对生成连贯、非冗余、可执行的研究任务组合具有显著价值。

Abstract: Research agents can increasingly search literature, propose hypotheses, generate code, run experiments, and draft manuscripts from a single topic. However, a research project is not merely a larger task: it is a long-horizon agenda that must be advanced through multiple bounded tasks with distinct but related objectives, parallel alternatives, and dependency-aware sequences. Existing single-task systems often treat the project as one oversized task, produce a flat set of vague or overlapping tasks, or leave task boundaries and execution order to manual coordination. We introduce Project2Task, a graph-guided project-level planning layer for autonomous research. Given a project brief, it represents candidate contributions as innovation atoms and organizes them in a directed lineage graph. A lightweight Bernoulli block-model objective selects among horizontal, vertical, and hybrid portfolio decompositions. Project2Task then generates bounded tasks with explicit contribution ownership, repairs overlaps and missing execution fields, and emits dependency-aware task contracts that specify objectives, inputs, expected artifacts, evaluation requirements, boundary constraints, dependencies, and execution order. The contracts are independent of any particular downstream research executor and support integration of task outputs into a coherent project-level result. On a benchmark of ten project briefs yielding roughly 30 tasks, manuscript-based portfolio evaluation gives Project2Task an average quality score of 7.15, compared with 4.58 for the Brief Baseline and 5.31 for the Topic-only Setting. Integrating its contracts with AutoResearchClaw increases average downstream task accuracy from 0.536 to 0.759. These results demonstrate the value of explicit project-to-task planning for producing coherent, non-redundant, and executable research-task portfolios.

</details>


### [98] [TriQua: Reconciling Granularity and Context in Factuality Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05228)
*Jin Liu,Steffen Thoma,Achim Rettinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TriQua框架，通过自适应事实建模（简单断言用标准三元组、复杂断言用带辅助语境限定符的超关系事实）来缓解"分解-验证"范式下原子事实丢失语境与宽泛陈述缺乏粒度之间的权衡；配套提出TriQuaScore量化事实性评分，实验表明其与人工标注高度一致，并在证据化事实验证中优于现有基于分解的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM事实性评估的"分解-验证"范式存在根本性权衡：原子事实（一个句子只表达一个信息单元）往往缺失关键语境信息，而更宽泛的陈述又缺乏精确评估所需的粒度。这种两难困境使得现有方法在事实检索和验证的准确性上受限。

Method: TriQua框架采用自适应的事实建模方式：简单声明提取为标准三元组，复杂声明则表示为超关系事实，通过附加辅助语境限定符（qualifiers）来保留必要上下文。验证过程直接在具体三元组和限定符上标注具体错误，提供细粒度的可解释性。同时提出TriQuaScore用于量化这些结构化事实单元的事实性。

Result: 实验表明：TriQuaScore与人工标注的事实性评分高度对齐；TriQua在分解质量上表现稳健；在基于证据的事实验证任务中，TriQua优于现有的基于分解的框架。

Conclusion: TriQua通过灵活调节事实表示粒度，在保留原子性和保留必要上下文之间取得了有效平衡，为LLM事实性评估提供了更精确且更具可解释性的分解-验证方案。

Abstract: The "decompose-then-verify" paradigm for LLM factuality evaluation faces a fundamental trade-off: atomic facts, i.e., one sentence conveying one unit of information, often omit essential context, while broader statements lack the granularity needed for precise assessment. To address this, we introduce TriQua, a framework that flexibly models facts based on their complexity. Simple claims are extracted as standard triples, while complex claims are represented as hyperrelational facts by attaching auxiliary contextual qualifiers. This adaptive structure preserves the necessary context for accurate retrieval and verification without sacrificing atomicity. Furthermore, TriQua's verification process directly annotates concrete errors within specific triples and qualifiers, providing fine-grained explainability for error detection. Alongside the framework, we propose TriQuaScore to quantify the factuality of these structured fact units. Empirical evaluations show that TriQuaScore strongly aligns with human annotated factuality scores, TriQua achieves robust decomposition quality, and outperforms existing decomposition-based frameworks in evidence-based fact verification.

</details>


### [99] [Coherence-Oriented Dream Scene Visualisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05233)
*Azra Açıl,Simon Colton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV)系统，该系统将文字梦境描述转化为包含四个时间序列面板图像的视觉化呈现。系统先用大语言模型将梦境描述拆分为四个时间顺序部分，再用文本到图像模型生成每个部分的图像，并通过重新生成机制确保图像与文本匹配及序列间的视觉连贯性。在DreamBank的50个梦境可视化样例上使用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL进行评估，报告了画质、保真度和连贯性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梦境情感强烈但难以用语言有效传达，需要一个系统将文字梦境描述自动转化为视觉化图像序列，帮助人们更好地理解和分享梦境内容。

Method: DSV系统由两部分组成：首先使用大语言模型将梦境描述拆分为四个按时间顺序排列的部分；然后使用文本到图像模型为每个部分生成图像，并通过视觉连贯性维持机制确保序列间的图像一致性，同时系统会重新生成任何与文本不匹配的图像。

Result: 在DreamBank的50个梦境描述可视化结果上进行了评估，采用CLIP、DINOv2和Qwen2-VL视觉语言模型作为客观度量工具，报告了图像质量、保真度（图像与文本匹配度）和连贯性（序列内图像一致性）三个维度的评估结果。

Conclusion: DSV系统成功实现了从文字梦境描述到四帧时间序列图像的可视化转换，通过LLM拆分情节、T2I生成图像及重生成纠正机制，在质量、保真度和连贯性方面均取得了可量化的评估结果。

Abstract: Dreams can be emotionally intense but difficult to communicate. We describe the Dream Scene Visualiser (DSV) system which turns written dream descriptions into a temporal sequence of four panel images visualising the dream. This starts with a large language model prompted to split a dream description into four chronological parts. Then a text-to-image model produces images for each part with visual coherence maintained across the sequence, and DSV regenerates any image not suitably matching the text. We evaluate DSV over 50 visualisations from dream descriptions in DreamBank, and report quality, fidelity and coherence results via objective measures employing the CLIP, DINOv2 and Qwen2-VL vision-language models.

</details>


### [100] [Search2Skill: Skill Distillation Beyond Knowledge Boundaries Via Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05245)
*Muyang Ye,Tian Lan,Feihu Jiang,Yongshi Ye,Wuyunsiqin,Bin Zhu,Qianghuai Jia,Zhao Xu,Weihua Luo,Ye Wang,Jinyang Zhang,Longyue Wang,Lingfeng Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Search2Skill框架，通过自动化识别LLM代理的能力缺口、搜索外部知识源弥补缺口、并将检索证据提炼为结构化可复用技能，使代理在专家领域实现自进化。采用基于rubric的强化学习方案联合优化搜索时机、搜索方式与技能生成，在八个专家级领域上优于搜索增强和轨迹式技能学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自进化技能方法仅从模型自身的参数化知识或执行轨迹中构建技能，受限于模型已知的知识边界。然而专业领域的约定俗成和标准流程往往超出这一边界，难以仅凭代理自身引出。

Method: 提出Search2Skill框架，包含三个关键步骤：（1）自动识别代理的能力缺口；（2）搜索外部知识源以弥补缺口；（3）将检索到的证据蒸馏为结构化、可复用的技能。整个框架由基于rubric的强化学习方案优化，联合改进何时搜索、如何搜索以及如何生成技能。

Result: 在来自三个基准的八个专家级领域上，Search2Skill在流式评估和保留评估两种协议下均一致优于搜索增强基线和基于轨迹的技能学习基线。进一步分析表明，性能提升来自技能抽象而非原始检索证据，且习得的技能可跨模型规模迁移。

Conclusion: Search2Skill通过引入外部知识源突破了模型自进化的知识边界限制，验证了技能抽象的价值以及技能的跨模型可迁移性，为LLM代理在专业领域的持续进化提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Reusable skills, which encapsulate the procedural knowledge required to solve real-world professional tasks, offer LLM-based agents a path toward self-evolution in expert domains. Existing self-evolving skill methods construct skills internally from the model's parametric knowledge or trajectories, and are therefore bounded by what the model already knows. However, the domain conventions and standard procedures underlying professional skills often lie beyond this boundary and are hard to elicit from the agent alone. To address this issue, we therefore propose a novel framework, Search2Skill, that automatically identifies the agent's capability gaps, searches external sources to address them, and distills the retrieved evidence into structured, reusable skills. Specifically, Search2Skill is optimized by a rubric-based reinforcement learning scheme that jointly improves when to search, how to search, and how to generate skills. Experiments on eight expert-level domains from three benchmarks show that Search2Skill consistently outperforms both search-augmented and trajectory-based skill-learning baselines under both streaming and held-out evaluation protocols. Further analyses show that the gains arise from skill abstraction rather than raw retrieved evidence, and that the acquired skills transfer across model scales.

</details>


### [101] [LUNAR: Benchmarking Personalized Large Language Models on UNiversal User BehAvioR Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05246)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yongzhi Tong,Zelin Fu,Pengde Zhao,Yanmei Jiang,Jiang Feng,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LUNAR是首个基于纵向应用交互历史评估LLM跨域行为个性化能力的基准，覆盖衣、食、住、行四大日常领域。实验表明行为日志对深度个性化是必要但非充分条件，证据选择、跨域整合和隐私控制是关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化LLM基准主要依赖文本人格描述或孤立的行为信号，缺乏对跨域行为个性化的评估。跨域行为个性化要求响应必须基于异构的日常活动，而现有基准无法覆盖这一需求。此外，构建此类基准面临数据稀疏和隐私问题。

Method: LUNAR采用多阶段由粗到细的合成流水线，基于真实行为模式进行构建，以支持可扩展的基准构建并缓解数据稀疏和隐私问题。通过保真度分析验证与真实行为分布的一致性。实验覆盖19个主流LLM，对比不同上下文长度、模型规模、检索策略（直接检索细粒度行为记录 vs 压缩记忆）以及个性化深度与隐私保护之间的权衡。

Result: 保真度分析表明LUNAR比其他合成基准更贴近真实行为分布。实验发现：访问行为日志是必要的但非充分条件；更多上下文和更大模型不保证更好性能；直接检索细粒度行为记录始终优于压缩记忆；更强的个性化可能以牺牲隐私保护为代价。

Conclusion: LUNAR揭示了证据选择、跨域整合和隐私控制是个性化LLM面临的核心挑战，为未来个性化LLM研究提供了新的评测基准和方向指引。

Abstract: Existing personalized LLM benchmarks primarily rely on textual personas or isolated behavioral signals, providing limited evaluation of cross-domain behavioral personalization, where responses must be grounded in heterogeneous daily-life activities. To address this gap, we introduce LUNAR, the first benchmark for evaluating how LLMs personalize responses from longitudinal app interaction histories across universal daily-life domains, including clothing, food, housing, and mobility. To support scalable benchmark construction while mitigating data sparsity and privacy concerns, LUNAR uses a multi-stage coarse-to-fine synthesis pipeline grounded in real-world behavioral patterns. Fidelity analyses show closer alignment with real behavioral distributions than other synthetic benchmarks. Experiments on 19 mainstream LLMs show that access to behavioral logs is necessary but not sufficient for deep personalization: neither more context nor larger models guarantees better performance; effective personalization depends on selecting and integrating relevant evidence across domains. Direct retrieval of fine-grained behavioral records consistently outperforms compressed memory, while stronger personalization can come at the cost of privacy protection. These findings identify evidence selection, cross-domain integration, and privacy control as key challenges for personalized LLMs.

</details>


### [102] [WorldClaw: Agentic 3D Open-World Generation at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05248)
*Chunchao Guo,Jinpeng Li,Yang Li,Zilong Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorldClaw是一个完全智能体驱动的、从粗到细的开放世界3D场景生成框架。它通过规划智能体将文本提示转化为结构化场景规格，然后从全局一致的地形基础逐步细化，最终生成具有空间连贯性、丰富局部内容和可编辑实例级资产的大型3D世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从开放式文本生成大规模、可自由探索的3D世界仍面临巨大挑战，因为系统需要同时保持全局空间连贯性、丰富的局部内容，并生成适合后续编辑和复用的显式资产，现有方法难以同时满足这些要求。

Method: WorldClaw采用完全智能体驱动的粗到细框架：规划智能体将文本提示转化为包含区域、地形、资产、材质和空间关系的结构化规格；随后构建全局连贯的地形基础（语义布局、可复用资产、生成或程序化材质、区域感知高度场）；对细节要求高的区域生成地形条件化组合、重建可编辑纹理网格并恢复其地形放置位置；基于渲染的智能体进一步细化地形、物体、外观和接触关系。

Result: 在多样化的开放世界提示下，WorldClaw能够生成具有连贯空间组织、视觉吸引力强的局部内容以及可编辑的实例级资产的大规模场景，同时保持一致的全局地形结构。

Conclusion: 通过分层智能体协作和粗到细的生成策略，WorldClaw有效解决了开放世界3D生成中全局一致性与局部丰富性、可编辑资产之间的权衡问题，实现了高质量且可用于下游编辑的大规模场景生成。

Abstract: Generating large-scale, freely explorable 3D worlds from open-ended text remains challenging because a system must jointly maintain global spatial coherence, rich local content, and explicit assets suitable for downstream editing and reuse. We present WorldClaw, a fully agentic, coarse-to-fine framework for open-world 3D scene generation. Planning agents translate a text prompt into a structured specification of regions, terrain, assets, materials, and spatial relations. WorldClaw then builds a globally coherent terrain foundation from semantic layouts, reusable assets, generative or procedural materials, and a region-aware height field. For detail-demanding regions, it generates terrain-conditioned compositions, reconstructs editable textured meshes, and recovers their placement on the terrain; render-based agents further refine terrain, objects, appearance, and contacts. Across diverse open-world prompts, WorldClaw produces large-scale scenes with coherent spatial organization, visually compelling local content, and editable instance-level assets while preserving a consistent global terrain structure.

</details>


### [103] [Posture and Sustainment Optimization Under Adversarial Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05256)
*Amelie Norris,Alyssa Lee,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了联合作战规划中预承诺态势（Pre-commitment posture）问题，即冲突场景尚未明确时将军事资产分配到战区位置。作者提出了场景加权对抗鲁棒态势优化引擎，包括CEV优化器和其扩展RobustCEV，并通过在印太基地环境中的三项实验验证了其相对贪婪基线的显著效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前军事资产态势分配依赖贪婪启发式算法，该算法只最大化价值而忽视地理覆盖，且在面对针对高战略价值位置的对手时结构性地脆弱。这是一个形式上未解决的联合行动规划关键问题。

Method: 将态势与可持续性分配（PSA）问题建模为资产、战区位置和时间步上的有限时域马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）。提出两个优化器：(1) 复合期望值（CEV）优化器，通过最大化威胁场景分布上的场景加权期望态势效率来分配资产；(2) RobustCEV扩展，迭代对抗一个根据观测到的放置更新目标分布的贝叶斯对手。

Result: 在20个资产和5个战区的印太基地环境中：(1) 贪婪基线因地理覆盖不足遭受25.1%的永久态势效率惩罚，在价值相关性对抗威胁下遭遇57.3%的场景加权就绪度崩溃；(2) CEV优化器在威胁分布带地理信号时比贪婪恢复最高19.8%效率，5-20个场景即可捕获大部分收益；(3) RobustCEV在自适应对手使用欺骗性威胁先验时相对朴素优化器恢复最高158%效率。所有发现经配对t检验与Bonferroni校正及两层方差分解验证。

Conclusion: 性能差距是放置策略的结构性属性而非采样伪影。CEV和RobustCEV优化器在面对地理信号威胁和自适应对抗威胁时显著优于贪婪基线，为军事态势规划提供了形式化的优化框架。

Abstract: Pre-commitment posture, the assignment of military assets to theater locations before conflict scenarios resolve, is a critical and formally unsolved problem in joint operational planning. Current practice relies on greedy heuristics that maximize value and ignore geographic coverage and are structurally vulnerable to adversaries that target high-strategic value locations. This paper presents a scenario-weighted adversarially robust posture optimization engine for the Posture and sustainability allocation (PSA) problem, modeled as a finite-horizon Markov Decision Process over assets, theater locations, and time steps. We introduce the Composite Expected Value (CEV) optimizer, which places assets by maximizing scenario-weighted expected posture efficiency over a distribution of threat scenarios, and the RobustCEV extension, which iterates against a Bayesian adversary that updates its targeting distribution in response to observed placement. Across three experiments in an Indo-Pacific basing environment with 20 assets and 5 theater locations, we demonstrate that: (1) the greedy baseline incurs a permanent 25.1% posture efficiency penalty due to geographic under-coverage and a 57.3% scenario-weighted readiness collapse under value-correlated adversarial threat; (2) the CEV optimizer recovers up to 19.8% efficiency over greedy when the threat distribution carries a geographic signal, with a curated set of 5 to 20 scenarios sufficient to capture the majority of this gain; and (3) the RobustCEV extension recovers up to 158% efficiency relative to a naive optimizer when an adaptive adversary employs a deceptive threat prior. All findings are validated using paired t-tests with Bonferroni correction and two-level variance decomposition, confirming that the performance gaps reported are structural properties of placement strategies rather than sampling artifacts.

</details>


### [104] [OrchestraBench: Evaluating Multi-Agent Orchestration Failure Modes, Recovery, and Decomposition Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05263)
*Yidian Chen,Yingzi Gu,Natan Vidra,Spurthi Setty,Sharon Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OrchestraBench是一个针对多智能体编排框架的故障诊断基准，通过可控的、可重现的故障注入机制评估流水线失败、恢复与分解能力，引入级联半径和按故障模式恢复率等新指标，并通过控制链机制探针揭示了不同路由策略与恢复模式的系统性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准只报告任务准确率，无法诊断流水线为何失败、级联在哪一步开始、哪个路由决策导致了崩溃。随着多智能体编排框架从演示走向生产，缺乏对失败原因和恢复机制的细粒度评估手段成为关键瓶颈。

Method: 在模板化的企业工作流上构建受控、种子可重现的故障注入测试平台。提出级联半径（cascade radius）和按故障模式恢复率（per-failure-mode recovery）作为主要指标；使用带bootstrap置信区间和配对检验的方式比较路由策略。设计26例金标诊断集（关键字/标志路由器 vs 意图推理路由器），并利用真实Claude智能体在可验证的算术依赖链上执行控制链机制探针，涵盖五个MAST模式，同时进行跨模型（Sonnet/Opus/Haiku）与跨领域（贷款审批工作流）的鲁棒性验证，以及可信状态修复消融实验。

Result: 关键字/标志路由器在具有误导性或缺席表面标志的对抗案例上得分为0%，而意图推理模型路由器得分100%并与oracle一致。五个MAST模式呈现三层故障处理能力：工具故障完全恢复（1.0）、模糊委派部分恢复（0.30）、三种潜在或语义模式完全无法恢复（0.0）。该排序在重构成贷款审批工作流及跨Sonnet/Opus/Haiku模型时保持稳定，但绝对比率随上下文变化。盲目重试会复现潜在故障并延长检测时间；级联半径随流水线深度增加（深度3-7时均值从0.9增至4.7）；可信状态修复消融表明表面上的遏制收益主要来自可信状态信号而非自主检测。

Conclusion: 检测与归因是故障遏制的前提条件；级联随流水线加深而扩大；恢复能力的差异在不同模式和上下文间具有结构稳定性。作者强调这些结论属于控制链机制探针的结果，不应等同于领域工作负载层面的普适性声明。

Abstract: Multi-agent orchestration frameworks are moving from demos to production, yet benchmarks typically report task accuracy without diagnosing why a pipeline failed, where a cascade began, or which routing decision caused the breakdown. OrchestraBench evaluates failure, recovery, and decomposition through a controlled, seed-reproducible failure-injection harness over templated enterprise workflows. It introduces cascade radius and per-failure-mode recovery as primary metrics and compares routing policies with bootstrap confidence intervals and paired tests. On a 26-case gold-labelled diagnostic, a keyword/flag router scored 0% on adversarial cases with misleading or missing surface flags, whereas an intent-reasoning model router scored 100%, matching the oracle. Controlled mechanism probes with a real Claude agent over a verifiable arithmetic dependency chain revealed three failure-handling tiers across five MAST modes: tool faults recovered fully (1.0), ambiguous delegation recovered partially (0.30), and three latent or semantic modes never recovered (0.0). This ordering persisted when the computation was reframed as a loan-approval workflow and across Sonnet, Opus, and Haiku, although absolute rates shifted with context. Blind retry reproduced latent faults and increased time to detection, indicating that detection and attribution are necessary for containment. Cascade radius increased with pipeline depth (mean 0.9 to 4.7 across depths 3-7). A trusted-state repair ablation showed that apparent containment gains primarily came from the trusted-state signal rather than autonomous detection. These results are controlled-chain mechanism probes, not domain-workload claims.

</details>


### [105] [Agentic self-driving microscopy benchmarks support qualification but do not necessarily generalize to unseen tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05266)
*Nathan S Johnson,Ian Abshire*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基准测试和轨迹日志框架，用于评估大语言模型智能体在控制显微镜等科学表征工具时的架构选择，包括1-3个智能体的图拓扑、5种LLM、RAG参数等。研究发现这些基准适合资格认证、回归测试和直接比较，但基于代理架构训练的替代模型无法可靠预测智能体在新任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型智能体越来越多地被用于控制显微镜和同步辐射束线等科学表征工具，但这一领域尚处于起步阶段，缺乏成熟的智能体系统工程范式。研究人员在设计显微智能体时面临大量选择（LLM型号、智能体数量、职责划分、RAG参数等），且需要确保智能体不仅能正确执行已知任务，还能泛化到未见过的任务。因此需要一套系统化的基准和评估框架来指导这些设计决策。

Method: 开发了一个基准测试和轨迹日志框架，系统性地评估了多种智能体架构设计选择。具体包括：比较了1-3个智能体的图拓扑结构、5种不同LLM、RAG和上下文参数以及操作约束，在53个显微基准测试上进行评估。总共记录了105种智能体配置、1,949次独立测试运行和49,109次RAG检索。同时训练了基于架构和测试结果的替代模型（surrogate model）来检验其对新任务的泛化预测能力。

Result: 直接对比显示不同配置在延迟、token使用、成本和失败模式上存在明显差异。然而，基于智能体架构和测试结果训练的替代模型无法可靠预测智能体在新任务上的性能。这表明当前异构的测试套件适用于资格认证、回归测试、诊断和直接比较，但不支持任务无关的全局配置模型。

Conclusion: 基准测试在智能体开发中有明确价值（资格认证、回归测试、诊断、直接比较），但当前测试基准无法支持对未见任务性能的全局预测。这意味着研究者需要谨慎使用基准结果来推广到新任务场景，可能需要更丰富的任务覆盖和更精细的元学习方法来建立跨任务的配置泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly being developed to control a wide range of scientific characterization tools including microscopes and synchrotron beamlines. Research into agentic control of physical infrastructure is nascent and there are few well-established paradigms for how to engineer an agentic system. There are many choices to make when designing a microscopy agent, including the choice of LLM, the number of agents to use, agent responsibilities and delegation rules, retrieval-augmented generation parameters, and more. When designing and optimizing an agentic microscope controller, researchers not only want to ensure that the agent can correctly perform known tasks but also that the agent can generalize to new tasks that it has not encountered before. In this study, we develop a benchmark and trace-logging framework that reveals a) how different choices of agent architecture impact performance at microscopy tasks and b) the limitations of benchmarks for predicting if a particular agent will perform well on unseen microscopy tasks. The framework was used to evaluate one-, two-, and three-agent graph topologies, five LLMs, RAG and context parameters, and operational constraints across 53 microscopy benchmark tests. In total, 105 agent configurations, 1,949 individual test runs, and 49,109 RAG retrievals were recorded. Direct comparisons showed clear differences in latency, token use, cost, and failure mode between configurations. However, surrogate models trained on agent architecture and test results did not reliably predict an agent's performance on new, unseen tasks. These results show that these benchmarks are useful for qualification, regression testing, diagnosis, and direct comparison, but the current heterogeneous test suite does not support a task-independent global configuration model.

</details>


### [106] [CASCADE: An Agentic Regulatory Network Framework for Patient-Data-Validated Downstream Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05359)
*Jose A. Bird*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CASCADE是一个基于ARACNe调控网络并通过MCP接口暴露的agent框架，用于预测基因扰动的下游转录效应。作者提出了一种反事实验证方法——利用TCGA患者肿瘤数据中焦点基因拷贝数扩增作为敲低反向的剂量代理，来检验预测的下调方向是否与真实表达一致。MYC预测在三种癌症类型中显示高度一致（72%-90%），优于随机基线，且通过PAM50亚型对照并在METABRIC独立队列中复现。但相比MSigDB精选基因集基线，并未显示对已有公共知识的显著超越。扩展到15个基因后发现验证效果具有基因特异性：增殖机制调控因子大多通过，而谱系身份转录因子和CCND2持续失败。此外，对LLM agent调用MCP工具的真实性做了基准测试，35个查询中本地模型达到71.4%精确匹配，更大模型达85.7%，但两个模型在模糊查询上都会自信地默认错误扰动类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对基因扰动预测工具的验证仅检查预测基因是否为已知癌症基因（成员资格验证），缺乏对预测方向是否符合真实生物学方向的功能性验证。作者希望提出更严格的、基于真实患者数据的方向一致性验证方法，同时评估agent实际调用MCP工具的能力。

Method: 使用TCGA中焦点基因拷贝数扩增（amplified vs non-amplified）作为敲低（knockdown）逆过程的剂量代理，将CASCADE预测的敲低目标方向与实际扩增肿瘤中差异表达方向对比一致性。对MYC在BRCA/COAD/STAD三种癌症及METABRIC独立队列中验证，并设置随机置换基线和PAM50亚型对照。扩展到15个额外基因进行泛化测试，同时用Fisher精确检验对比MSigDB基因集。另设独立基准，测试LLM agent是否能正确将自然语言查询映射为CASCADE的真实MCP工具调用。

Result: MYC验证在三种癌症中一致率分别为90.0%、72.0%、85.7%（p<0.0013），METABRIC复现87.2%，远超置换基线。但Fisher精确检验显示CASCADE并未显著超越MSigDB已有知识基线。15个基因的扩展验证呈基因特异性：增殖机制调控因子多通过，谱系身份转录因子和CCND2失败。MCP grounding基准中，本地模型71.4%精确匹配，更大模型85.7%，混淆查询上两模型均固执选择错误扰动类型，且定位修复未能解决（触发条件从未出现）。

Conclusion: CASCADE在特定的增殖相关基因（如MYC）上具备可靠的方向性预测能力，但并非普适有效；其性能未超越现有公共基因集知识，凸显了"新颖性"维度的匮乏。LLM agent在MCP工具调用上整体可用但存在系统性的模糊查询失误，且这类失误难以通过针对性修复解决，提示需要更根本的设计改进。

Abstract: CASCADE is an agentic framework that predicts downstream transcriptional effects of gene perturbation from precomputed
  ARACNe regulatory networks, exposed via MCP. Prior work validates such tools by checking whether predicted genes are
  known cancer genes (membership); we instead test whether the predicted direction of change matches reality, using
  focal-gene copy-number amplification as a dosage-based proxy for the inverse of knockdown against real TCGA patient
  tumor data.
  For MYC, CASCADE's predicted knockdown targets show strong concordance with real amplified-vs-non-amplified tumor
  expression across three cancer types (BRCA: 90.0%, COAD: 72.0%, STAD: 85.7%; all p<0.0013), well above permutation
  baselines, surviving a PAM50 subtype control and replicating in an independent cohort (METABRIC, 87.2%). Compared
  against curated MSigDB gene-set baselines via Fisher's exact test, CASCADE's accuracy is not shown to exceed existing
  public knowledge of MYC- or E2F-driven biology, though its gene-specific direction-calling clearly outperforms a naive
  uniform guess.
  Extending to fifteen additional genes, validation proves gene-specific rather than universal: proliferation-machinery
  regulators mostly replicate, while lineage-identity transcription factors and one cyclin-D paralog (CCND2)
  consistently fail, a pattern we discuss as a hedged, post-hoc hypothesis.
  We separately benchmark whether an LLM-based agent correctly grounds natural-language requests into CASCADE's real MCP
  tool calls. Across 35 queries, a documented local model reaches 71.4% exact match (85.7% for a larger model); schema
  and gene-alias failures are resolved by scale or server-side correction, but both models confidently default to the
  wrong perturbation type on ambiguous queries, a failure a targeted fix could not resolve because its trigger condition
  never occurs.

</details>


### [107] [Counterfactual Analysis via Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05367)
*Zonghao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用GPT-3.5大语言模型进行反事实分析，特别是在在线借贷场景中评估不同利率方案下的反事实投资回报率。研究发现提示工程可将GPT的R²从1.97%提升至2.84%，接近梯度提升回归的3.48%，且GPT在生成反事实ROI时展现出逻辑连贯性和因果推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反事实分析在决策支持中具有重要价值，但传统机器学习方法在反事实预测中存在局限。本文旨在探索大语言模型（尤其是GPT-3.5）能否作为一种有效的工具应用于反事实分析，特别是在在线借贷领域评估不同利率方案的投资回报率，以期为决策提供更优的预测手段。

Method: 本文采用三个阶段的方法：首先评估GPT-3.5的预测性能并将其与先进的机器学习算法（如梯度提升回归）进行对比；其次通过提示工程（prompt engineering）优化GPT的预测效果；最后利用优化后的GPT在替代利率集合下生成反事实ROI，并检验其逻辑一致性和因果推理表现。

Result: 实验结果表明：提示工程显著提升了GPT的预测性能，R²从1.97%提高到2.84%，接近梯度提升回归所达到的3.48%；GPT在生成反事实ROI时表现出逻辑连贯性和因果推理能力，验证了其作为反事实分析工具的可行性。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在反事实分析中展现出巨大潜力，尤其是在在线借贷等实际业务场景中。研究为LLM在更广泛的预测和决策制定场景中的应用提供了有力证据，表明通过适当的提示工程，LLM可以成为反事实分析的有效补充工具。

Abstract: Counterfactual analysis aims to predict potential outcomes under hypothetical scenarios, offering valuable insights for decision-making. This paper investigates the application of large language models (LLMs), specifically the GPT-3.5 model, for counterfactual analysis. We focus on the online lending context, where the counterfactual return on investment (ROI) is crucial for evaluating different interest rate schemes. We begin by assessing the predictive performance of GPT and comparing it with advanced machine learning algorithms. The results show that prompt engineering can significantly enhance GPT's predictions, with the R-squared increasing from 1.97% to 2.84%, closely approaching the 3.48% achieved by gradient-boosted regression. Subsequently, we utilize GPT to generate counterfactual ROIs under a set of alternative interest rates. GPT exhibits logical coherence and causal reasoning in its responses. The findings underscore the potential of LLMs as effective tools for counterfactual analysis in online lending, suggesting broader applications for LLMs in various predictive and decision-making contexts.

</details>


### [108] [DoctorAgents: an agentic framework to iteratively refine AutoML pipeline for small clinical temporal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05375)
*Ruilin Wang,Bo-Hong Wang,Elizabeth Kourbatski,Jun Bai,Hegang Chen,Ziyang Song,Gilles Boire,Marie Hudson,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DoctorAgents，一个基于智能体AI的框架，通过专门的大语言模型智能体（生成、验证、精炼）将临床小数据的AutoML从穷举搜索转变为推理驱动的精炼，利用文本梯度下降反向传播自然语言反馈进行针对性更新，在多种临床任务上优于现有AutoML基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床机器学习在高风险医疗决策中潜力巨大，但小规模、异质性和时间复杂性限制了可靠部署。现有AutoML系统依赖对预设空间的暴力搜索，缺乏显式推理和记忆，无法有效应对临床小数据的挑战，亟需更高效、更有针对性的自动化ML构建方法。

Method: DoctorAgents采用多智能体架构，包含三个专门化LLM智能体：生成智能体负责构建端到端ML流水线，验证智能体评估流水线性能，精炼智能体基于文本梯度下降反向传播自然语言反馈进行针对性优化更新，从而避免全局穷举搜索。

Result: 在多种临床任务上的实验表明，DoctorAgents持续优于已有的AutoML基线方法，同时生成更具可解释性的任务特定表示。

Conclusion: 将AutoML从穷举搜索重构为推理驱动的精炼是解决临床小数据ML建模难题的有效路径，智能体化LLM框架能够显著提升流水线构建的效率与质量。

Abstract: Clinical machine learning (ML) has the potential to support high-stakes medical decision-making, but reliable deployment is often constrained by scarce, heterogeneous, and temporal complexity. Developing effective ML pipelines for such data remains time-consuming and error-prone, while existing automated machine learning (AutoML) systems only partially address this challenge because they largely rely on brute-force search over predefined spaces and lack explicit reasoning and memory. We therefore reformulate AutoML for small clinical data from exhaustive search to reasoning-driven refinement. We propose DoctorAgents, an agentic AI framework that autonomously constructs and optimizes end-to-end ML pipelines through specialized large language model (LLM) agents for generation, validation, and refinement. DoctorAgents backpropagates natural-language feedback through textual gradient descent to perform targeted updates without exhaustive search. Experiments across diverse clinical tasks show that DoctorAgents consistently outperforms established AutoML baselines while producing more interpretable task-specific representations.

</details>


### [109] [C$^3$PO: Evaluating Cross-Modal Composition and Counterfactual Performance in Omnimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05381)
*Swapnanil Mukherjee,Agyeya Negi,Tanuja Ganu,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C³PO基准测试，包含3,404个跨视频、音频、图像和文本的样本，评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在信息组合和反事实冲突两种能力上的跨模态推理表现。研究发现人类准确率达88.64%，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅达73.17%，且86-95%的失败源于模态主导性——模型过度依赖单一模态（尤其是文本，注意力占比达87-95%）。中间层注意力熵可作为正确性的预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs虽然能处理多种感官输入，但其推理严重偏向主导模态，导致跨模态推理脆弱。缺乏一个系统性的基准来诊断跨模态推理何时以及为何失败。

Method: 构建C³PO基准，包含3,404个样本，覆盖视频、音频、图像和文本四种模态。通过25个逻辑模板的全自动流水线生成，采用配对的IC（信息组合）/CC（反事实冲突）结构和四层设计来精准诊断推理失败。并通过注意力探针分析模型内部注意力分布。

Result: 人类准确率88.64%，最佳模型Gemini-3.1-Pro仅73.17%，开源模型在冲突场景下严重退化。86-95%的失败源于模态主导性，模型将87-95%注意力集中在文本上。中间层注意力熵与正确性相关，持续探索成功，过早崩溃失败。同等复杂的模板间存在56个百分点的准确率差距。

Conclusion: 多模态感知并不保证鲁棒的推理能力，架构需要支持持续的跨模态注意力以避免过早收敛到单一模态。性能取决于模态在冲突解决中的结构角色，而非模态组合本身。

Abstract: Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) can process diverse sensory inputs, yet their reasoning remains heavily biased toward a dominant modality, resulting in brittle cross-modal reasoning. We introduce C$^3$PO, a benchmark of 3,404 samples spanning video, audio, image, and text, evaluating two abilities: information composition (fusing dispersed evidence) and counterfactual conflict (resolving deliberate contradictions). C$^3$PO's paired IC/CC structure and four-tier design enable targeted diagnosis of when and why cross-modal reasoning fails. Built through a fully automatic pipeline using 25 logically grounded templates, C$^3$PO reveals that while humans achieve 88.64% accuracy, the best model (Gemini-3.1-Pro) reaches only 73.17%, with open-source models collapsing under conflict. Through attention probes, we find 86-95% of failures stem from modality dominance: models commit to one modality while ignoring contradictory evidence, concentrating 87-95% of attention on text. Mid-layer attention entropy predicts correctness-sustained exploration succeeds, premature collapse fails. The 56-point accuracy gap between equally complex templates reveals that performance depends on modalities' structural roles in conflict resolution, not combinations. These findings show multimodal perception does not guarantee robust reasoning; architectures must enable sustained cross-modal attention to avoid premature

</details>


### [110] [Adaptive Arena-based Contestable Argumentative Network-of-Experts for Open-Ended Care Plan Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05391)
*Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen,Hoang-Loc Cao,Phuc Ho,Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen,René Richard,Hung Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CANOE是一个多智能体神经符号框架，通过五个模块（复杂度评估、自适应团队招募、基于A-QBAF的角色论证计算、人在环路质询、护理计划综合）来解决护理计划协调中异构信息整合的透明性和安全性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 护理计划协调需要跨多个专业学科整合异构的临床、功能和心理社会信息，单一的LLM流水线无法以透明或安全的方式完成此任务。

Method: 提出CANOE框架，包含五个模块：复杂度评估、自适应团队招募、基于竞技场式量化双极论证框架（A-QBAF）的角色论证计算、人在环路质询机制以及护理计划综合。角色专业化的智能体为候选干预措施生成支持和攻击性论证，冲突通过竞技场式冲突消解来解决，随后可接受性得分在论证图中传播。护理规划者可以接受、拒绝、编辑或添加论证，框架会确定性地重新计算最终计划。

Result: 在Discharge Me!和MedicalRAG数据集上使用ROUGE-L、AlignScore、MEDCON F1、FKGL和LLM-as-a-judge进行评估，结果显示医学微调模型在临床正确性和安全性方面表现最强，而CANOE的论证结构提供了忠实的解释和人工可质询性。

Conclusion: CANOE通过论证结构实现可信解释和人工可质询性，在临床正确性和安全性上达到了最强表现，为护理计划协调提供了透明、可交互的多智能体解决方案。

Abstract: Care plan coordination demands synthesizing heterogeneous clinical, functional, and psychosocial information across multiple professional disciplines, where monolithic LLM pipelines cannot perform in a transparent or safe manner. We present CANOE (Contestable Argumentative Network-of-Experts), a multi-agent neuro-symbolic framework that addresses these limitations through five modules: complexity assessment, adaptive team recruitment, role-based argumentative computation via an Arena-based Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Framework (A-QBAF), human-in-the-loop contestation, and care-plan synthesis. Role-specialized agents generate supporting and attacking arguments for candidate interventions; conflicts are resolved through arena-based clash resolution before acceptability scores propagate across the argumentation graph. Care planners may accept, reject, edit, or add arguments, and the framework will deterministically recompute the final plan.
